{
 "title": "MV-1: the smallest honest test of \"taught to learn\" — v7-min stream, target-blind development, source-disjoint deployment (2026-09-19 to 09-28)",
 "days": 10,
 "gpu_hours": 1600,
 "design_md": "# MV-1: the smallest honest test of \"taught to learn\"\n\nv7-min stream · target-blind development · source-disjoint deployment · 16 GPUs · 2026-09-19 (Sat) to 09-28 (Mon)\n\nDesigner 1 of 4, angle MINIMUM-VIABLE: one mechanism change at a time, strict baselines inside every life, falsifiers written before launch. Nothing from this experiment enters the ICLR 2027 paper (numbers frozen 09-14 / 09-20); it is the first experiment of Rohin's thesis programme.\n\n---\n\n## 0. One screen\n\n- **Question.** Does a child that was *developed* before deployment (Stage 1: structure bootstrap from a target-blind source; Stage 2: a 256-episode persistence classroom with a critic that never gives answers) improve **faster per unit of experience** during autonomous continual learning in a gym held out **by source**, than an undeveloped child running the **identical** mechanism?\n- **Primary comparison.** D (developed) vs B (regular), matched seeds, K = 4, same v7-min stream, same gate, same panels, parent-absent at deployment. Readout at cycle 16 (deployment episode 512) on report panel v2, each arm paired against its own frozen reference (adapter OFF).\n- **Baselines inside every life.** Adapter-OFF paired probe (frozen reference); TEXT probe (frozen model + the life's own brief + read-only RECALL over the life ledger, no weights); the 8-program reference panel at 3 checkpoints.\n- **Mechanism control.** A = same runner, v6 stream (DONE allowed, no states). The single mechanism change (no end token + harness state boundaries + review pinned into the next state) is measured against it.\n- **Ablations (wave 2, pre-registered rule).** C (bootstrap birth, no Stage 2 — launched in wave 1 if the birth is admitted on time), C' (bootstrap + Stage 2 gym **without** the critic), E (text-memory agent living the life), k-stream pilot (development-only, fixes protocol for the next experiment).\n- **Not run.** Plasticity; conversations; clone merge / head-node; dedup-in-compiler (unless F7 fails); branching as a confirmatory arm; bootstrap-lived composition control (optional wave 2).\n- **Verdict rule.** Falsifiers F1–F7 (section 7), each with cycle, metric, margin, K seeds. No sentence enters any paper unless its falsifier is met; nulls are reported as \"not at this dose/horizon\", never as \"no\".\n- **Budget.** ~1,600 GPU-h executed inside a 16-GPU × 10-day lease (3,840 GPU-h held). 14 lives in wave 1, up to 8 in wave 2.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Hypothesis, alternatives, and why this is the minimum\n\n**H1 (taught to learn, minimal form).** Let L_X(t) = ON(t) − OFF for arm X at deployment cycle t on report panel v2 (OFF = that arm's own frozen reference: clean base for A/B/E, bootstrap base B1 for C, schooled base S_i for D/C'). Then L_D(16) − L_B(16) ≥ +0.02 in ≥ 3 of 4 matched seed pairs. This is the \"second derivative\" (rate of improvement on a task distribution the parent never showed) in its smallest measurable form.\n\n**Alternatives the design rules out by construction, not by argument:**\n\n| alternative explanation | how it is killed |\n|---|---|\n| the frozen model could already do it | OFF probe at every checkpoint, per arm |\n| text memory explains it (M4) | TEXT probe every 128 in every life; arm E lives the whole life on text only |\n| Stage 1/2 pre-trained the deployment task (\"the classroom pretrained it\") | Stage 1 source and Stage 2 gym contain zero CompilerGym content (rule game only); deployment is the first gym contact for every arm |\n| the mechanism change itself (v6→v7) produced the effect | arm A (v6 stream) vs arm B (v7 stream), everything else identical |\n| entry-level offset masquerading as learning | OFF(0) per arm reported separately; the claim is about L, never about OFF_D > OFF_B |\n| selection on the report panel (F1) | gate on panel v1b, report on panel v2, reference on the 8-panel; overlap is a hard error |\n| heavy-tailed single probes (SEQ-002: one same-adapter pair differs by 0.065) | 2-rep gate; cycle-16 readout = mean of the ep 448 and ep 512 probes; min/max reported |\n| bootstrap/parent contamination (Codex STOP, COORD 857) | fail-closed provenance guard with negative tests before any birth; every life carries a manifest |\n\n**Why this is the minimum.** The thesis needs three things and nothing else: (i) a mechanism in which reviews can be written and remembered (v7-min), (ii) a development treatment that is target-blind, (iii) a deployment readout held out by source with the three baselines. Branching, conversations, clone merges and plasticity are each a second mechanism change and multiply compute; they are deferred with triggers (section 11). The C/C'/E ablations attribute a positive D-vs-B result; they are not needed to *falsify* it, so they run second.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Measured state this design inherits (cell counts, sources)\n\n- Recipe lock-in: adapter-ON plateaus 0.4878 (= birth recipe applied once) and 0.5291 (a six-pass routine) on the 8-panel; -Oz 0.522, -O3 0.341; exploring frozen model 0.463–0.488; life-probe OFF mean 0.471–0.474 (SEQ-001/003; COORD 911).\n- **Derived check (mine, from `research_notes/disjoint_panel_v1.json` precheck fields + SEQ-003 -Oz values):** the birth recipe applied once scores **0.2495** on disjoint panel v1; R2 seed2's final and mid-life adapters scored **0.2496 / 0.2495** there, both reps identical (SEQ-006). The frozen model scores 0.257/0.254; -Oz 0.2745. So the recipe-locked write reproduces the birth recipe on programs it never saw and that recipe is *worse* than exploration on this panel by ~0.007. Pre-registered prediction for the pending re-probe: every 0.4878-plateau adapter (R3 501/503, R4 600/602/603) scores 0.2495 ± 0.003 on v1. This is why the new lineage's birth prompt carries no recipe (section 3.1).\n- Efficiency declines in every arm (chunks-to-best 3–4 → 6–9); contrast phrases and backtracks → ~0; first-ACT JS shift between cycles 0.02–0.13; taught vs untaught do not separate (SEQ-005).\n- Life-level harm: ungated R2 4/9 lives with ≥1 harmful pair; RP 1/3; gated R3 0/6 but post-selection; late-life candidates in R3 500 collapsed to 0.19–0.34 and the gate refused all six (SEQ-001/002).\n- Behavioural gate catches with passing scores: R3 500@64, 501@704, 505@288 (COORD 899).\n- Noise: SD(OFF) 0.0092/0.0087 on the 8-panel; same-adapter replicate SD 0.0065 (n=70) with a 0.065 outlier; disjoint v1 replicate SD 0.006–0.007 (SEQ-001/002/006).\n- Echo metric: 0.454 after a brief vs 0.322 pre-brief vs 0.354 unbriefed controls; \"rehearsal 0.50–1.00\" withdrawn (SEQ-003).\n- Ritual onset 160–224 in 8/9 unparented lives; parented 288/192(+remission)/256, returns and persists (COORD 546).\n- Bootstraps: v1/v2 QUARANTINE_TASK_EXPOSED; v3 DEV_UNVERIFIED_PROVENANCE; no canonical clean child exists (COORD 795, 813, 857).\n- Verified code fact that this design must fix: `gpu/launch_bootstrapped_life.sh` seeds `sleep_0000/adapter`, but `run_life_v2.py` retrains every sleep **from the clean base** (`train_adapter.py` builds a fresh LoRA; `latest_adapter` picks the newest DONE), so a bootstrap adapter would be **replaced at the first sleep (episode 32)** and would affect only the first 32 episodes' generation. A bootstrap that is meant to persist must become the lineage's birth base (section 3.3).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Mechanisms built (and what is deliberately unchanged)\n\n### 3.1 v7-min stream — the one mechanism change (new lineage root; child continuity rule)\nBuilt as `organism_v6/run_life_v3.py` (+ `batch_loop`/`state`/`sleep_compile` edits behind a `--stream {v6,v7}` flag so arm A runs the same code with the v6 stream).\n- **No end token.** `DONE` removed from `_MARK`, `_MD_MARK`, and the birth prompt; a situation ends only when the harness ends it (16-chunk budget). CLOCK stays as information. Tokens after the last ACT are \"white space\" thought and are logged (`chunks_after_last_act`).\n- **Harness state boundaries.** A STATE = one situation (one program instance) with budget 16. Wake batches of 8 run as 8 **slot-streams**: slot s's state k+1 pins slot s's state k review. Ledger rows gain `state_id`, `slot`, `situation`; a harness row `kind=\"state_end\"` records the state's best score, acts, and its review text.\n- **Review pin (minimal recurrent CoT).** The last NOTE the child wrote in state k becomes `LAST REVIEW (state k)` at the head of state k+1's context (`render_context` change). The harness never asks for a review; the birth prompt (same for all arms) says once: \"each situation has a fixed number of chunks (CLOCK); before it ends, write your review as a NOTE — it is the first thing you will see next.\" Review presence and quality are therefore *unprompted relative to the harness* and comparable across arms.\n- **Compile change (organizational, dumb).** For every state with ≥1 valid outcome, one review exemplar enters the corpus: `Program {eid}. After {n} actions (best {best}), my review: {review}`. Only child-authored text grounded by a committed action/public outcome (Codex, COORD 674 item 2). Everything else in `compile_sleep` is unchanged (wins, pathways, recoveries, contrasts, THINK-call principles and brief; exact-prefix dedup only).\n- **Birth prompt.** No concrete recipe: the `ACT: -mem2reg, -sroa, -gvn, -simplifycfg` example and the INVALID hint's four pass names are replaced by a neutral one-line glossary of the 124 valid pass names (documentation, not a recipe). Applied to **all** new arms (A included), so A vs B differs only in the stream. Consequence: the new arms' 8-panel numbers are not comparable to R2–R5 and are not used as such.\n- **Arm A (v6 stream).** Same runner, `--stream v6`: DONE allowed, no `[STATE]`/`LAST REVIEW`, no review exemplars. Everything else (gate, panels, prompt, writer, budget) identical.\n\n### 3.2 Gate hygiene, applied to every gated arm (not a difference between arms)\n- Gate on **panel v1b** (section 3.7), never the report panel; base measured once on the gate panel at birth; floor = max(gate_base, best committed gate mean) — no floor decay (from `--gate-panel`, COORD 979).\n- **2-rep score gate**: candidate probed twice on the gate panel (seeds 4242, 5242); commit iff both reps ≥ floor − 0.02. This is the minimum-viable form of \"confirm before trusting one 8-episode draw\" (SEQ-002 ASK); the provisional-commit-with-revert form is the alternative if Codex prefers it (0.5–1 ED swap).\n- **Behavioural canary redefined under no-DONE:** acts per situation on the gate probe ≥ 0.5 × base's (the \"act once and idle\" analog of \"act once and stop\"). Used for A and B alike.\n- **Paired format canary:** parseable-ACT rate of the candidate ≥ base's − 0.10 on the same gate probe (replaces the absolute 0.5).\n- Rejections logged by reason (SCORE_REP1/SCORE_REP2/ACTS/FORMAT); no recovery path (a life that rejects its late writes stays on its last good adapter — safe and informative).\n\n### 3.3 Birth bases (the bootstrap must live in the base, verified gap in section 2)\n- `organism_v6/merge_birth_base.py`: `peft merge_and_unload()` of a LoRA into Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (snapshot a09a3545…), saved as a local bf16 checkpoint (~15 GB); `V6_MODEL` points the life's vLLM and trainer at it. One merge for the bootstrap (B1); for D/C' a second merge of the Stage-2 final committed adapter into B1 (schooled base S_i, one per seed). Rank 8, alpha 16; the merge is exact up to bf16 rounding (disclosed).\n- Adapter-OFF for a merged-base life = the merged base with no life LoRA. This makes ON − OFF the deployment learning gain in every arm, and OFF(0) the arm's entry level (Codex: separate entry level from the acceleration).\n- Deployment starts a **new life dir and a new ledger**: Stage 1/2 carry over in the weights only. No waking brief and no parent brief at deployment (parent-absent, scaffolding removed at the phase boundary).\n\n### 3.4 Fail-closed provenance guard (precondition for any birth; Codex STOP COORD 857)\n`organism_v6/provenance.py`: birth manifest with base id + HF snapshot hash; bootstrap corpus sha + every source-ledger sha + the runner/renderer code shas that produced them; author model + revision; `mechanism_version` = sha over `organism_v6/*.py`; writer/compiler path; scanner version; `exposure_domains`; parent bytes hash (Stage 2); ancestor chain; merge receipt (birth-base sha). `admit()` refuses launch on any missing field, any ancestor in the quarantine list (`R2_B_seed3`, `bootstrap_v1/v2/v3`, any CompilerGym-exposed dir), or any exposure domain other than `rulegame` for a clean birth. Negative tests: copied directory without receipts → refuse; self-declared manifest → refuse; quarantined ancestor → refuse. Launch scripts call `admit()`; the runner refuses to start without a passing manifest. Codex reviews the schema (ASK, D1); until Codex ratifies, C/D are labelled \"guard-passed, Codex review pending\".\n\n### 3.5 Stage 1: target-blind structure bootstrap (v4 corpus, fresh source)\n- **Source:** 7 frozen-model lives (arm A, no adapter, no parent, no write) of 256 episodes in the base rule game (10 rules), run under the v7-min runner so every stored prompt render is in the v7 dialect. Zero CompilerGym content, zero parent text — no leak surface, no classroom commit-rule defect (M11), no answer-aware parent (M8).\n- **Render:** `bootstrap_corpus.py` with an updated `AUTHOR_BOOT` (v7 dialect: states, LAST REVIEW, no DONE), Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct-AWQ as **author** (named as author, never as parent), composition v2: META-FLOW 0.4 / REVIEW 0.15 / OPENING 0.2 / CONTRAST 0.15 / FULL 0.1; `acts_ok` guard drops any rendering that adds or changes an ACT; target ≈ 500 rows (v3 had 470).\n- **Checks before training:** ritual metrics on the corpus must show 0 flags (v3: recipe share 0.26, note Jaccard 0.11); `leak_scan` over q and a for LLVM pass tokens must hit 0; composition and source shas into `corpus_meta.json`.\n- **Write:** `train_adapter_v21` (chat-masked, response-only loss, lr 3e-5, 2 epochs, r8, seeded). Then merge → birth base **B1**. Disclosure: Stage 1 uses the v2.1 masked writer, Stages 2–3 use the v1 bare-text writer (+seed); both named in every table.\n- **Control corpus (optional wave 2):** the same source rendered with `--mix v1` (lived-heavy) → B1-lived → C_lived, if GPUs allow.\n\n### 3.6 Stage 2: persistence classroom with a critic (D only; C' = same gym, no critic)\n- **Gym: rule game v2 (`rulegame_v2.py`).** Compound rules (AND/OR of two primitives; parameterized thresholds) generated per eid; families split into training vs exam (held out **by family**). Persistence by construction: the QUIZ is accepted only after ≥ 6 TRY probes in the state (else `INVALID: probe at least 6 times before the quiz (you have N)`); score = quiz accuracy. Payoff arrives late; quitting early is impossible to reward. Curriculum of 256 training instances persisted at launch.\n- **Critic (14B, pinned revision, `parent_brief` v4-persistence, bytes frozen and hashed before any D seed launches):** fires at **every** sleep (8 sleeps), sees ritual metrics + persistence stats (TRY count before quiz, distinct hypotheses NOTEd, early quiz attempts) + 4 raw samples; writes ≤ 8 lines of process critique that rewards continued, changing attempts and specific reviews; never names a rule, descriptor or answer; word-level `leak_scan` with the compound-rule descriptor list, `SCANNER_VERSION` recorded, fallbacks logged per life; same core lesson across sleeps (repetition), shown at every wake, with the rehearsal tail (Rohin's ruling; the echo metric with its null is the diagnostic, Codex COORD 616).\n- **Mechanics identical to deployment:** v7-min stream, sleep every 32, v1 writer, gate on a 16-instance held-out-family exam (2 reps, base = B1 measured at Stage-2 birth), probes every 64 (descriptive). After sleep 256 the final committed adapter is merged into B1 → S_i; the Stage-2 dir is sealed and its receipt appended to the manifest (exposure `rulegame_v2`, parent bytes sha, brief shas, gate log).\n- **Fading:** step-fade at the phase boundary (critic text present at every Stage-2 wake, absent at deployment). Retention readout: echo metric of deployment first NOTEs against the last Stage-2 brief, with B's first NOTEs against the same brief as the null.\n\n### 3.7 Panels (CPU, `cgym_eval --ref`, hours; Rohin signs off before build)\n- **Report panel v2 (primary, held out by source):** 12 programs from datasets no curriculum, the 8-panel, or v1 use (candidates: poj104-v1, anghabench-v1, github-v0 — whichever are installed), kept only where -Oz reduction ≥ 0.10 and (-Oz − birth recipe) ≥ 0.03 so situation-dependent choice can register; IR size 200–20k instructions; seeded draw; `disjoint_panel_v2.json` records recipe/-Oz/-O3 per program. Fallback if no new dataset installs: split v1 by source (npb → gate, blas/opencv/tensorflow → report) and say so.\n- **Gate panel v1b:** v1 minus the three tensorflow programs (≤ 0.034 -Oz headroom) plus three npb/blas replacements with ≥ 0.05 headroom; fallback v1 as-is.\n- **8-panel reference:** ON/OFF at ep 0, 512, 1024 only (cross-history reference; same-source siblings disclosed).\n- References recorded once per panel: frozen model (≥3 reps, seed 777), birth recipe, -Oz, -O3, and B1 / S_i frozen references (2 reps) on the utility GPU.\n\n### 3.8 Probe conditions (every life)\n| probe | context | RECALL | adapter | cadence |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| ON | birth prompt only, fresh ledger | probe ledger only | life LoRA on its base | every 64 (v2); ep 0/512/1024 (8-panel) |\n| OFF | same | same | none (arm's base) | same |\n| TEXT | birth prompt + the life's current waking brief (+ parent brief in Stage 2) | read-only copy of the life ledger | none | every 128 (v2); every 64 for arm E |\nAll probes: seed 777 (per-rep offsets), budget 16, `alive` clock frozen in probe prompts, `seed` forwarded in `__call__` so sleep THINK-calls are seeded (M3).\n\n### 3.9 Instruments (CPU, from disk)\n`efficiency_markers.py` extended: state grouping, tokens-to-best, acts/situation, chunks after last ACT; `review_detectors.py`: presence (a NOTE after the final ACT), components 0–4 (error attribution / success attribution / scope-generalization / next-time plan), specificity (names a program or state other than the current one), consecutive-review Jaccard in a slot-stream; `echo_null.py` for post-withdrawal echo; `analysis_tables.py` with arm, base id, manifest id, panel v2 series, L(t), OFF(0), harm, rejections by reason, restarts, exposure; per-program v2 table; ritual under the frozen 09-09 definition (no rehearsal exemption at deployment; both definitions in Stage 2). A review gallery (10 random states per life at cycles 2, 8, 16) is part of the deliverable — Rohin wants to see the text.\n\n### 3.10 Deliberately unchanged\nv1 bare-text writer (lr 1e-4, 3 epochs, max_len 512) + `--seed` and model/revision/seed in `train_meta.json` (v1.1 = reproducibility only); rank 8 from birth; temperature 0.7; budget 16 chunks × 400 tokens (token-sufficient regime, never forced, logged); wake batch 8; sleep every 32; probe every 64; `compile_sleep` except section 3.1; RECALL keyword retrieval; the waking brief.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Arms and baselines\n\n| arm | stream | birth | Stage 2 | deployment | K | node | wave | role |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **A** | v6 (DONE) | clean base | none | CompilerGym, gated v1b, report v2 | 3 | Y | 1 | mechanism control for B |\n| **B** | v7-min | clean base | none | same | 4 | Y | 1 | regular agent; primary comparator |\n| **C** | v7-min | B1 (bootstrap) | none | same | 3 | X | 1 (if birth admitted by D4) | Stage 1 alone |\n| **D** | v7-min | B1 → S_i | rule game v2 + critic, 256 eps | same, parent-absent | 4 | X | 1 | developed agent; primary treatment |\n| **E** | v7-min, arm \"A\" (no LoRA ever) | clean base | none | same life, TEXT probes every 64 | 3 | Y | 2 | text-memory agent (gym-is-the-world baseline) |\n| **C'** | v7-min | B1 → S'_i | rule game v2, no critic | same | 3 | Y | 2 | isolates the critic from gym practice |\n| **k-pilot** | v7-min + [BRANCH]/[JOIN] k=4×16 vs k=1×64 | clean base | none | 128 eps, development-only | 1+1 | Y | 2 | fixes probe protocol, branch-compile rule, throughput |\n| C_lived (optional) | v7-min | B1-lived | none | same | 3 | Y | 2 | composition control (structure vs lived) |\n\nMatched seeds: D_i, B_i, C_i, C'_i share the wake generation seed (1000+i) and therefore the same training-program order; pairs are formed by seed. Two parents/authors named separately in every table: 32B-AWQ (Stage-1 author), 14B (Stage-2 critic). No R2–R5 child is resumed (all are disposable development lives; Codex firewall COORD 813).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Gyms and traits\n\n- **Deployment gym: CompilerGym llvm-v0, instruction-count reduction.** Kept per Rohin's ruling; the protocol inside it is the benchmark. Traits it can register: persistence (acts after a non-improving outcome, distinct ACTs per situation, chunks-to-best), reflection (review detectors), situation-dependent choice (per-program v2 table; recipe-vs--Oz gap ≥ 0.03 required for inclusion).\n- **Stage-2 classroom: rule game v2 (persistence).** Target-blind; hidden dynamics; payoff after ≥ 6 probes; environmental feedback = the box's True/False answers; the critic is one feedback channel; the child's own reactions are what sleep writes (Rohin's DEFINITION). Disclosed limitation: the primitives are textbook rules (M11); the compound families and the ≥6-probe structure are what make it a persistence gym, and no score claim is made on it.\n- **Stage-1 source: rule game v1, frozen model.** Not a gym for learning — a target-blind source of the child's real contexts and grounded episodes for the structure renderings.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Measurement protocol\n\n- Unit of analysis = life; cell counts; every number names arm, base, panel, checkpoint age; pairs by seed for D−B, C−B, C'−D.\n- Per cycle (32 episodes): paired ON/OFF on v2 every 64; TEXT every 128; gate log per sleep (2 reps, canaries, floor); ritual metrics per window under the frozen definition; efficiency (chunks-to-best, tokens-to-best, acts/situation, chunks after last ACT); markers (backtracks, strategy switches, contrast/self-eval regex — noisy, secondary); review detectors per state; JS shift of the first-ACT distribution between consecutive cycles (day-one gate).\n- Curves reported: L_X(t) for every life; OFF_X(0) entry level; TEXT_X(t) − OFF_X; D−B by seed; harm = pair < −0.03 on v2 (life-level counts); rejection streaks; ritual onset window; review quality rate per cycle; echo after withdrawal (D) vs null (B).\n- Provenance: every life dir has `manifest.json` (arm, base sha, mechanism_version, panel shas, gate params, parent bytes sha, runner args, COORD snapshot hash at launch). Receipts mirrored to the laptop nightly (nodes have no direct link).\n- Two horizons: cycle 16 (ep 512) is the pre-registered decision cycle for all falsifiers; 1024 is the full-life readout where the calendar allows; lives not at 1024 are reported censored at their age (M2).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Falsifiers (written before launch; cycle N, metric, margin, K seeds)\n\n| id | claim tested | cycle | metric | margin | K | if not met |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **F1** (thesis) | developed learns faster | 16 (dep. ep 512; mean of ep 448 + 512 probes) | L_D − L_B on panel v2 | ≥ +0.02 in ≥ 3/4 seed pairs (≈1.5 SD per pair; P(≥3/4 under null) ≈ 0.002) | 4 vs 4 | no \"developed agents learn faster\" sentence anywhere; reported as a null at 256-episode Stage 2 + 512-episode deployment with the horizon caveat |\n| **F2** (efficiency) | developed reaches its best faster | 16 | chunks-to-best on v2 ON probes, cycles 13–16 | D below B by ≥ 20% in ≥ 3/4 pairs | 4 vs 4 | no efficiency sentence |\n| **F3** (persistence retained) | the instilled trait survives deployment without the critic | 13–16 | backtracks/episode and distinct ACTs/episode in deployment wakes | D ≥ 2 × B in ≥ 3/4 pairs AND D ≥ 0.25 backtracks/ep (baseline ~0 late-life in every v6 arm) | 4 vs 4 | \"persistence\" not claimed; reported as marker counts |\n| **F4** (bootstrap installs the review habit) | Stage 1 alone changes review quality | 1–2 (first 64 dep. episodes) | review quality rate = states with ≥3 components + specificity + Jaccard < 0.5 | C and D ≥ 0.5 in ≥ 3/4 (3/3) lives; B < 0.25 in ≥ 3/4; ritual onset in C/D ≥ 2 windows later than B | 3+4 vs 4 | bootstrap reported as \"did not install the habit at 500 rows\" |\n| **F5** (safety) | gated writes do not harm on unselected programs | every probe to 1024 | pairs < −0.03 on v2 vs own OFF | 0 harmful lives in B, C, D (life = unit) | 11 lives | arm flagged; rejection logs and harm ages reported; no safety sentence |\n| **F6** (weights, not text) | the learning gain is in the weights | 16 | ON − TEXT on v2 | ≥ +0.02 in ≥ 3/4 lives of an arm | per arm | that arm's gain is described as \"text-explainable\"; E's curve is the comparator |\n| **F7** (day-one gate for the mechanism) | v7-min changes behaviour between cycles at all | 2–8 | median JS shift of first-ACT distribution between consecutive cycles | ≥ 0.15 in ≥ 3/4 B lives (v6 baseline 0.02–0.13); A reported alongside | 4 (+3) | downstream score comparisons declared uninterpretable; wave 2 switches to dedup arm + k-pilot (section 8, D5 rule) |\n\nPre-registered predictions for pending data (section 9): 0.4878-plateau adapters score 0.2495 ± 0.003 on v1; -Oz mean on v1 = 0.2745.\n\n---\n\n## 8. GPU budget and calendar\n\n**Lease.** Two new 8×A40 nodes (or one 16-GPU allocation) from 09-18 through 09-29. Request by Tue 09-15 (provisioning via `gpu/v2_bootstrap.sh` + CompilerGym venv tarball relay takes ~half a day; environments captured per M10). Check `colossus bm lease list` trailing-30-day headroom first (node 1 ≈ 216 h + node 2 ≈ 336 h + new ≈ 528 node-hours in window). Nodes cannot reach each other: arms are node-local; receipts relay via laptop. Node X (parent side): GPU0 = 14B critic server; GPU1–7 lives. Node Y: GPU0 = utility (32B author, references, TEXT proxies, smoke, k=1 comparator); GPU1–7 lives.\n\n**Executed GPU-hours (life ≈ 54 h for A, 62 h for v7 CompilerGym lives, 16 h per Stage 2, 50 h for E):**\n\n| phase | GPU-h |\n|---|---|\n| wave 1 lives: A 3×54, B 4×62, C 3×62, D 4×(16+62) | 908 |\n| Stage-1 source 7×10, author render 12, bootstrap train 1, merges 2 | 85 |\n| 14B critic server held D2–D5 (idle mostly) | 96 |\n| wave 2: E 3×50, C' 3×78, k-pilot 2×29 | 442 |\n| utility: references, TEXT proxies, smoke/dry runs, rubric scoring | 40 |\n| optional C_lived 3×62 + render 6 (not in total) | (192) |\n| **total executed** | **≈ 1,570 (≈ 1,600 with slack); lease held 3,840** |\n\n**Calendar (Sep 10 is a Thursday; D1 = Sat 09-19).** Pre-window 09-15..18 (code only, no launches, recommended): if used, everything below shifts one day earlier and D reaches 1024 in-window.\n\n- **D1 Sat 09-19.** Nodes live; ENV captured. Rohin signs off the two environment/benchmark drafts (rule game v2; panel v2 criteria) before build. Build: v7-min runner (`--stream`, states, review pin, review exemplars, `--gym`, `--gate-panel` 2-rep + acts + paired-format canaries, `--report-panel`, `--text-probe-every`, birth-base env), `provenance.py` + negative tests, panel precheck script, v1.1 trainer seed, `seed` forwarding, frozen `alive`. Evening: smoke (arm A, 16 episodes, both gyms, ~2 h) on utility GPUs — run-order discipline: cheap trace before any long run. ASK→Codex: guard schema, gate form (2-rep vs provisional/revert), bootstrap writer (v2.1 masked).\n- **D2 Sun 09-20.** Morning: panel v2 and v1b frozen (CPU precheck on node; references recorded); dry run (arm B, 32 episodes, 2 sleeps, gate, probes, ~3 h). Afternoon: launch **A×3, B×4** (node Y GPUs 1–7). Launch **Stage-1 source lives** ×7 (node X GPUs 1–7, frozen model, rule game v1, 256 eps, ~10 h). Critic server up (node X GPU0, pinned revision). Build rule game v2 + critic prompt v4 (bytes hashed) + SCANNER_VERSION; smoke.\n- **D3 Mon 09-21.** Source lives done; receipts hashed. Author server (32B-AWQ) on node X GPU7: render v4 corpus (~10 h), leak-scan, ritual-on-corpus (must be 0 flags), composition logged. Evening: train bootstrap adapter (~1 h), merge → **B1**, guard `admit()` + negative tests → manifest; post to COORD. Old node 2 lease ends: pull R5 / re-probe / brief-baseline receipts to laptop.\n- **D4 Tue 09-22.** Launch **C×3** (node X GPUs 5–7, base B1, deployment). Launch **D×4 Stage 2** (node X GPUs 1–4, base B1, rule game v2, critic every sleep, gated on held-out-family exam; ~16 h). Codex reviews the manifest (async).\n- **D5 Wed 09-23.** Morning: Stage 2 done → merge each final committed adapter → S_1..S_4; manifests appended; launch **D×4 deployment** (parent-absent). A finishes (~morning), B finishes (~evening). CPU: F7 on B, F5 to date, ritual onset, review detectors B vs A. **Wave-2 rule (executed by the daemon, no judgment):** if F7 met in ≥ 3/4 B lives → launch E×3 (Y1–3), C'×3 Stage 2 (Y4–6), k-pilot k=4 (Y7) + k=1×64 (Y0 when free); else → launch dedup arm B_dd×3 (new root: near-duplicate collapse per Codex's dedup protocol if it has landed, otherwise count-capped exact-template collapse) + E×3 + k-pilot, C' dropped.\n- **D6 Thu 09-24.** C at ~ep 800; D at ~ep 400. C' Stage 2 done → merge → C' deployment. Reference probes for B1 and S_i on v2 and the 8-panel (utility). Review gallery v1 (A, B).\n- **D7 Fri 09-25 (ICLR paper deadline).** Experiment unattended (daemon, marker-file resume). D crosses cycle 16 (~evening); C finishes. k-pilot finishes.\n- **D8 Sat 09-26.** **Cycle-16 readout: F1, F2, F3, F6 for D vs B** (pre-registered; two probes each). D reaches 1024 (~morning). E finishes. C' at cycle 16 (~evening).\n- **D9 Sun 09-27.** C' finishes. Full-life tables for A/B/C/D; F4 and F5 final; echo-after-withdrawal for D with B null; per-program v2 table; rejection logs; ritual under both definitions for Stage 2; rubric scoring (14B, secondary) of the review gallery.\n- **D10 Mon 09-28.** Decision packet for Rohin (recommendation + reason code; tables beneath); SEQ-numbered COORD entry with COORD snapshot hash; all receipts (ledgers, probes, gate.json, manifests, bases' shas — adapters excluded) pulled to laptop and hash-verified before the lease ends 09-29.\n\n**Cut order if anything slips** (never below K=3 for B and D): C_lived → k-pilot → C' → E → C (3→2, then characterization only) → A (3→2, then characterization only).\n\n---\n\n## 9. Decision points that pending data resolve\n\n| pending item (lands) | outcome | what changes in MV-1 |\n|---|---|---|\n| **DP1 disjoint re-probe F1b** (09-13/14) | gated finals ≥ base − 0.03 on v1 | nothing structural; F5 margin stays −0.03; gate design confirmed as the default |\n| | finals below base (first cell −0.005…−0.008; my prediction: all 0.4878-plateau adapters at 0.2495) | confirms recipe lock-in has zero/negative transfer → recipe removed from the birth prompt of all new arms (done in 3.1); the 8-panel is reference-only; v2 becomes the only claim panel |\n| | tensorflow programs compress differences | v1b replaces them (3.7); v2 requires ≥ 0.03 recipe-vs--Oz gap |\n| | panel v1 as the next test split? | No: v1(b) = gate panel; v2 = report panel; if no new dataset installs, split v1 by source and disclose |\n| **DP2 text-memory proxy M4** (brief_baseline.sh, ~5–10 GPU-h) | OFF+brief ≈ ON in ≥ 5/9 R2 lives | the current gain is text-explainable → E promoted to wave 1 (request +2 GPUs; else B drops to K=3 with D), TEXT probes stay every 128 |\n| | ON ≥ OFF+brief + 0.02 in ≥ 6/9 | weights carry the gain → E stays in wave 2 as designed |\n| | either | the \"equally capable text-memory agent\" for the finals is defined as arm E (brief + read-only RECALL over the life ledger during wakes and probes), superseding the brief-only proxy |\n| **DP3 R5** (disjoint gate, no parent; n bounded by node 2) | R5's unselected 8-panel numbers match R3's post-selection numbers (0 harmful) | gate result is real; 2-rep gate + acts canary kept as designed |\n| | R5 shows harmful 8-panel pairs while its gate panel stayed clean | gate protects only what it measures → widen v1b to 16 programs if headroom allows; F5 stays the report-panel readout |\n| | Codex ratifies provisional-commit/revert instead of 2-rep | swap the gate form (0.5–1 ED) before D2 launch; if ratified after launch, keep 2-rep for this experiment and disclose |\n| **DP4 R4** (gate + parent v3 to 1024 + re-probe) | no separation from R3 on efficiency/markers at 1024 (SEQ-005 trend) | prior on D−B lowered; critic v4 keeps the v3 core (contrast, scope, deviation) plus persistence; nothing structural changes |\n| | node-1 R4 contrast rise (5.2 → 10.0, n=3, young) holds and separates | critic v4's contrast lesson kept unchanged; the parenting hypothesis is worded around markers, not score |\n| | v7/conversations on RP/R4 children? | only smoke tests of code on frozen-model rule-game lives; no task-exposed child is resumed |\n| **DP5 R3 500–503 late-life logs** | \"gate refuses collapsing late writes\" holds across lives | no change; rejection-streak reporting kept; no recovery path |\n| | held adapters collapsed on v1 anyway | F5 is the safety readout; the gate is reported as partial protection |\n| **DP6 Codex answers** (guard schema; split hygiene; dedup protocol; three challenges) | guard schema ratified by D4 | C/D labelled clean; else \"guard-passed, review pending\" until ratified — no claim before |\n| | dedup protocol lands by D5 | dedup arm eligible for wave 2 (only if F7 fails, or on freed GPUs) |\n| | split hygiene ratified | not needed by MV-1 (Stage 2 is target-blind); enables CompilerGym-training-split classrooms in the next experiment |\n| **DP7 leak audit M8** | delivered briefs contain residual descriptors | critic v4 ships with the compound-descriptor word list + SCANNER_VERSION; fallback rates per life; \"never gives answers\" written as \"process-only by prompt and leak-scanned (vN)\" |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Risks (and the mitigation already in the design)\n\n1. **Lease/provisioning slip.** Request by 09-15; provisioning script exists; cut order in section 8; the design degrades to A/B/D at K=3.\n2. **Codex does not ratify the guard in time.** C/D run as \"guard-passed, review pending\"; no clean-child claim until ratified; if a defect voids the birth, C/D become development lives and the thesis test re-runs next lease. B and A are unaffected.\n3. **Harness bug found late (the M-series pattern).** Mandatory smoke (D1) and 32-episode dry run with two sleeps, gate and probes (D2) before any launch; marker-file resume; atomic writes; resumed lives flagged (minor 1).\n4. **F7 fails (behaviour flat, as in every v6 arm).** The score comparisons are declared uninterpretable in advance; the experiment still delivers the review-text gallery, ritual/marker contrasts, and a dedup arm in wave 2.\n5. **Heavy-tailed probes.** 2-rep gate; two-probe cycle-16 readout; min/max reported; SD from disk.\n6. **Score headroom on v2 too small (recipe ≈ -Oz, as on npb).** Inclusion rule requires ≥ 0.03 gap; F2/F3/F4 carry the mechanism story even if F1 is underpowered.\n7. **Rule-game face validity (M11).** Disclosed; no rule-game score claim; the claim is about deployment behaviour.\n8. **Disk.** ~120 GB of merged bases per node; check before D3.\n9. **The 32B author writes text that was never lived (M13).** Accepted design; described as \"32B-authored structure rendered over the child's real rule-game contexts, trained before birth\".\n10. **Paper deadline collides with D5–D7.** Wave-2 decision is a written rule executed by the daemon; analysis begins D8.\n11. **Reading the null as \"no\".** Stage 2 is 256 episodes and deployment is read at 512: the write-up says \"not at this dose/horizon\" by construction (horizon warning, IDEAS 09-07).\n12. **Two writers in one lineage (v2.1 for Stage 1, v1.1 for Stages 2–3).** Disclosed in every table; the alternative (bare-text bootstrap) loses the context-conditioned structure and is the optional composition control's job, not this experiment's.\n\n---\n\n## 11. Deferred, with triggers — and answers to Codex's asks\n\n- **Branching (k parallel streams merged by the child).** Wave-2 pilot only (k=4×16 vs k=1×64 vs B's k=1×16, 128 episodes, development-only): fixes the probe protocol (probe with k=1), throughput, and the branch-compile rule. **Answer to challenge (a):** only the JOIN state's committed pathway and its review compile; unchosen branch drafts are ledger rows `kind=\"branch_thought\"` that `select()` never reads. Confirmatory arm in the next experiment once wave-1 shows \"aligned but not great\".\n- **Conversations → opinions.** Not built (Codex counter unanswered; not needed for the thesis test). **Answer to (b):** a conversational opinion needs no new support class; it enters SLEEP only when restated in a later committed trace — the existing review-exemplar rule already covers that.\n- **One child, many clones / head-node parenting.** **Answer to (c):** MV-1 uses sibling forks (matched seeds from one birth) and never pooled clones; pooled-clone childhood is a separately labelled condition for the next experiment, reusing `classroom_round`'s merge with per-source provenance (provenance kept as a hygiene record, not a gain-attribution requirement — Rohin's \"thoughts carry the gain\").\n- **Near-duplicate collapse.** Wave-2 arm only if F7 fails or GPUs free; needs Codex's threshold/support-count protocol.\n- **Plasticity.** Off, as ruled.\n- **Bootstrap-lived control (C_lived).** Optional wave 2 from the same source (`--mix v1`); composition is secondary to \"does development change the curve at all\".\n- **Evaluator agent (LLM rubric over reviews).** Secondary descriptive table only; every falsifier uses world-side numbers or written regex detectors.\n\n## 12. Sign-offs needed before D1 builds\n\n- **Rohin:** rule game v2 (compound families, ≥6-probe quiz rule) and panel v2 criteria — environment/benchmark drafts require his edits before implementation; the birth-prompt recipe removal; the step-fade reading of repetition-vs-fading (critic text present at every Stage-2 wake, absent at deployment).\n- **Codex:** provenance manifest schema and negative tests; gate form (2-rep vs provisional/revert); bootstrap writer (v2.1 masked); whether the review-exemplar rule satisfies \"committed-branch-only SLEEP\".\n- **Lease:** two 8-GPU nodes 09-18 → 09-29, requested by 09-15 after a quota check.\n\nFiles this design touches (all under `/Users/rohing/dream-state/organism_v6/` unless noted): `run_life_v3.py` (new), `batch_loop.py`, `state.py`, `sleep_compile.py`, `train_adapter.py` (seed + meta), `model_backend.py` (seed forwarding), `bootstrap_corpus.py` (v7 AUTHOR_BOOT), `parent_brief.py` (v4 prompt, SCANNER_VERSION), `parent_backend.py` (descriptor list), `rulegame_v2.py` (new), `provenance.py` (new), `merge_birth_base.py` (new), `review_detectors.py` (new), `efficiency_markers.py`, `analysis_tables.py`, `echo_null.py`; `/Users/rohing/dream-state/research_notes/disjoint_panel_v2.json` and `disjoint_panel_v1b.json` (new); `/Users/rohing/dream-state/gpu/` launch scripts for the new arms.\n"
}

Decision packet (one screen)\n\n**Recommendation (reason codes in brackets).**\n\n1. **Retire the 8-program panel as a discriminating instrument; keep it only for continuity and harm.** [CEILING: -Oz 0.522 − birth recipe 0.4878 = 0.034 total headroom; the smallest arm-vs-arm difference in life-mean ON−OFF that n = 8 per arm can detect is 0.048 (n = 16: 0.034), computed from the between-life SD 0.033 of the nine finished R2 lives. Stage A pilot at n = 4 (fixes variance components and thresholds), Stage B at n = 8 (n = 16 if the quota increase lands). Hypotheses (falsifiable) and what this experiment is not\n\n**H0 (day-one gate).** Under the v7 stream the child's first-action distribution keeps changing between cycles (median JS shift ≥ 0.15 over cycles 8–32) where every finished v6 life went flat after cycle 1 (13/14 lives median < 0.15; SEQ-005).\n\n**H1 (first derivative on the training split).** A v7 life improves on the programs it revisits (per-program revisit curve slope > 0; per-cycle wake best slope > +0.001/cycle) where all 14 finished v6 lives have non-positive slopes (mean −0.0033/cycle; computed from disk).\n\n**H2 (markers survive).** Backtracks and contrast statements per episode stay ≥ half their early-life rate through cycles 16–32 (baseline 0/14), and review-shaped statements appear in ≥ 50% of states.\n\n**H3 (weights vs text).** The adapter carries gain the text memory does not: on the transfer panel, (ON − OFF) exceeds (text-memory − OFF) by ≥ 0.02 at ages 512 and 1,024.
