replicate) | — | — |\n| treated 0/8 harmful vs 4/9 | p = 0.053 | 0/12: p = 0.021 | 0/16: p = 0.010 |\n\n**Consequences.** (a) The 8-panel's total headroom (0.034) is below the n = 16 MDE: it cannot separate arms by gain. (b) The scoping's efficiency falsifier (\"≥ 20% lower chunks-to-best by cycle 16 across ≥ 3 seeds\") is underpowered as a mean test at n = 8 (MDE 33%); it is re-expressed as a per-life binary with a measured 1/14 baseline. (c) Harm is a safety readout, discriminating only at n ≥ 12 — so harm is pooled across all gated lives of the root writer (n = 24). (d) Everything that separates at n = 8 is categorical: markers off the floor, JS shift, positive slopes, late/never ritual, recipe unlock, review shape. **The mechanism set is therefore restricted to mechanisms hypothesized to move these categorical readouts** — the v7 stream, branching, near-duplicate collapse, a fading parent, and a persistence curriculum — plus the two baselines. Small-effect questions (writer, rank, parent size) are not run as confirmatory arms.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The benchmark protocol (the protocol lives inside the gym; the gym stays)\n\n### 3.1 Units, cycles, lives\n- Unit of analysis = **life** (or lineage root); pairs/checkpoints/writes are within-life series only (review M1). Every number names its child, arm, panel, probe cell, exposure (episodes reached), restarts, and node.\n- Life = 1,024 episodes (the horizon floor), sleep every 32 (32 cycles), budget 16 chunks per situation for every arm and every probe (token-sufficient regime; a thinking-budget arm would confound probes and is not run).\n- Wake batch 8; generation seeds derive from (episode id, tick, seed); sleep THINK-calls become seeded (instrument fix, §3.9).\n\n### 3.2 Panels and the sealed construction rule\n\n| panel | role | programs | cadence | selection |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **P8** (legacy report-by-identifier) | continuity with R2–R5; harm history; recipe-lock diagnostics | the 8 cbench programs | cells 1–2 every 64 | frozen; labelled \"held out by identifier; same-source siblings in curriculum; development-only\" |\n| **G** (gate) | selection only; never reported as gain | disjoint panel v1 (12: npb ×6, blas ×2, opencv ×1, tensorflow ×3); the three tensorflow programs (≤ 0.034 -Oz headroom) are replaced by the next three hash-ranked npb/blas programs unless the pending re-probe shows they discriminate (§9) | every sleep, candidate only; base once at birth | overlap with P8 or T is a hard error (already enforced for P8 in `run_life_v2.py:182-184`) |\n| **T** (transfer/report panel v2) | the report panel for gain, harm, first derivative; held out **by source** | 16 programs from a dataset none of the curriculum/P8/G uses (candidates in order: poj104-v1, github-v0, linux-v0, anghabench-v1 — whichever installs) | cells 1–2 every 128 (8 checkpoints); cells 3–4 at 256/512/768/1024 | sealed rule below |\n| **F** (finals family) | second derivative only | F_train 32 programs (deployment curriculum) + F_test 12 programs (probe) from a dataset disjoint from all of the above | finals stage only | sealed rule below |\n\n**Sealed rule (two independent no-model sealers, Fable and Codex, must agree; run on the node's CompilerGym venv, CPU only):** (1) enumerate the installed dataset manifest; (2) compute each program's reset-state IR instruction count and a content hash of the reset module; exclude any hash collision with the 67 curriculum programs, P8, G, and every previously inspected development program (the microassay plan's rule: URI disjointness is insufficient); (3) recipe-once score ≥ 0.02 (pass choice registers at all); (4) **search headroom** = best-of-256 random 3–8-pass sequences − recipe-once ≥ 0.10 (situation-dependent choice can register; -Oz and -O3 recorded as references but not used for selection); (5) sort by content hash and take the first qualifying 16 (T) / 44 (F). Panel JSON carries every reference (recipe, -Oz, -O3, best-of-256, base count). Per-program scores are reported raw and as **headroom captured** HC = (score − recipe)/(best-of-256 − recipe), clipped to [−1, 1] — a recipe-locked child scores HC = 0 by construction.\n\nWhy this matters: on panel v1 the recipe-to--Oz gap is npb 0.04/0.07/0.05/0.01/0.02/0.04, blas 0.02/0.07, opencv 0.00, tensorflow 0.00/−0.03/0.01 (SEQ-003 vs the panel's recipe precheck). A panel where the recipe already equals the compiler cannot show thinking as score.\n\n### 3.3 Four probe cells and the baselines\n\nEach probe checkpoint runs up to four cells on a panel, all with budget 16, seed 777 (+ panel-specific offset), fresh probe ledgers (probe rows never train):\n\n| cell | adapter | text memory | meaning |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| (1) OFF / no-mem | off | birth prompt only | frozen model = the **regular agent** (re-measured every checkpoint) |\n| (2) ON / no-mem | on | birth prompt only | **weights alone** (today's ON probe) |\n| (3) OFF / mem | off | birth prompt + this life's current waking brief (+ parent brief if any) + RECALL over a read-only snapshot of the life ledger | the **equally capable text-memory agent** given this life's own text |\n| (4) ON / mem | on | same memory | the **deployed agent as it acts** |\n\nReported per life: weights effect (2)−(1); text effect (3)−(1); interaction (4)−(3)−(2)+(1); as-deployed gain (4)−(1). Cells (1)–(2) at every checkpoint; cells (3)–(4) on T at ages 256/512/768/1024 (cost control).\n\n**Baseline agents.** (a) Adapter-OFF = cell (1) in every life. (b) **TEXT arm** = `run_life_v2 --arm A` lives at 1,024 episodes: the frozen loop with NOTEs, RECALL over its own ledger, and the waking brief written by `compile_sleep`'s THINK-call at every sleep, but no adapter (verified in code: the compile block runs for both arms; training is gated on arm B). Its \"learning\" probe is cell (3). Arm-A lives have only ever run 128 episodes (COORD ~68: \"R_A seeds 0–2 at ~105/128 wakes\"). (c) Regular agent = cell (1). (d) The brief-only proxy (`probe_adapter --brief-file`, `gpu/brief_baseline.sh`) remains the paper's retrospective text-memory proxy for finished lives; it is not the next experiment's baseline.\n\n### 3.4 First and second derivatives (definitions)\n\n- **d1-train (training-split learning curve, free from the ledger):** for each curriculum program p (67 programs, each visited ~15 times per life), the sequence of episode-best scores over visits v = 1..V; per life: mean over p of the slope of score vs visit, and the paired difference last-visit − first-visit. Also the per-cycle wake best slope (already in `efficiency_markers.py`; baseline 14/14 ≤ 0). The revisit-curve baseline for all finished lives is computed on node 2 before 09-21 (§8, Stage 0).\n- **d1-eff (efficiency):** actions-to-best per episode by cycle (new; comparable across DONE and no-DONE regimes) and chunks-to-best (continuity); under branching, all branch actions count (honest cost accounting).\n- **d1-T (transfer curve):** cell (2) on T at the 8 checkpoints → slope and (age 1024 − age 128).\n- **d2 (second derivative; finals):** the age-1,024 child is deployed as a new life on F_train for 256 episodes (8 sleeps, gate on G unchanged), probed on F_test at 64/128/192/256 with cells (1)–(4); per child: entry level (checkpoint 0) and slope of cells (2) and (4). Compared with a newborn of the same mechanism on F and with TEXT-on-F. Entry level and slope are reported separately (Codex: inherited competence ≠ learning acceleration).\n\n### 3.5 Per-life binary readouts and their measured baseline rates\n\n| id | readout (per life) | baseline among finished v6 lives | pass mark at n = 8 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| B1 | median JS shift over cycles 8–32 ≥ 0.15 | 1/14 | ≥ 6/8 (p = 0.002) |\n| B2 | backtracks/episode, cycles 16–32 ≥ 0.5 × own cycles 2–4 | 0/14 | ≥ 6/8 (p = 0.0004) |\n| B3 | contrast phrases/episode, cycles 16–32 ≥ 0.5 × own cycles 2–4 | 0/14 | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B4 | pooled chunks-to-best, cycles 12–20 ≤ 5.0 | 1/14 (R3 503 at 5.0) | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B5 | per-cycle wake best slope > +0.001/cycle (and revisit-curve slope > 0) | 0/14 | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B6 | first ritual window ≥ 320 or never | 1/12 | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B7 | recipe unlock: cell (2) modal first-action share on P8 ≤ 0.7 at ≥ half the checkpoints | arm means 0.86–0.90 (per-life table produced at Stage 0) | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B8 | harm-free: no pair (2)−(1) < −0.03 on T with ≥ 14 checkpoints | pooled readout, §3.6 | ≤ 2/24 harmful |\n| B9 | transfer gain: (2)−(1) on T at 1024 ≥ +0.03 | unknown (first cell −0.005/−0.008); set at pilot | ≥ 6/8 |\n| B10 | review shape: ≥ 50% of states at cycles 16–32 contain a review with ≥ 2 of {where wrong, what generalizes with scope, next time, what went well} | REF's NOTE stream scored by the same detector (pilot) | ≥ 6/8 |\n\n5/8 = \"suggestive, replicate before any sentence\"; 4/8 = null. With n = 16 the pass mark is ≥ 10/16 against ≤ 2/16 (p = 0.005).\n\n### 3.6 Harm, gate, provisional commit\n- Harm = pair < −0.03 on T (primary) and P8 (continuity), counted per life with exposure (checkpoints at risk) and a per-checkpoint hazard table (review M2). Because n = 8 cannot resolve harm rates, harm is pooled over all gated lives of the root writer (REF + V7-1 + V7-4m = 24 lives): ≤ 2/24 harmful = \"the gate keeps harm rare on a panel it never selected on\"; ≥ 5/24 = gate redesign before any further arm.\n- Gate (all gated arms): candidate ≥ max(base on G, best **confirmed** committed gate probe) − 0.02, plus the behaviour canary. **Provisional commit:** the candidate is mounted as PROVISIONAL and confirmed at the next sleep's gate probe (ON ≥ floor − 0.02) or reverted to the previous DONE adapter (REJECTED_UNCONFIRMED); the floor uses confirmed probes only; wake rows generated under a reverted adapter remain experience (nothing is deleted). Motivation: same-adapter replicates are heavy-tailed (one of 70 pairs differed by 0.065; SEQ-002). Pending Codex ratification (ASKs COORD:546, 896, SEQ-002).\n- Behaviour canary under no-DONE (chunks/episode becomes ~constant): candidate's actions per situation ≥ 0.5 × base's, AND mean distinct ACT strings per situation ≥ 2 (guards \"same action 16 times\" and \"zero-action thinking loops\"). For the v6-stream arms the legacy chunks/episode ≥ 0.5 × base canary is kept and both are logged.\n- Rejection log by reason is a first-class table (REJECTED_CANARY / SCORE / BREVITY / ACTS / UNCONFIRMED).\n\n### 3.7 Markers, review detector, audit, qualitative samples\n- Markers (regex, `efficiency_markers.py`): self-evaluation, contrast/scope, strategy switches, backtracks, recalls, notes. Known to be noisy (SEQ-005). **Audit rule:** before any marker count is reported, 50 randomly chosen (seeded) flagged chunks per arm are judged by the 14B parent model against a written rubric (prompt version logged) and by one human pass on 20 of them; detector precision must be ≥ 0.8 or the marker is reported only as \"detector-flagged text, precision X\".\n- Review-shape detector (new): counts, per state, the four review elements; per-state metrics: states per situation, review presence, references to an earlier state (\"in state 3 I…\"; the recurrent-CoT signature).\n- Ritual metrics computed per 32-episode window under **both** the frozen 09-09 definition and the 09-10 rehearsal-exempt definition, for every arm (review M6).\n- Echo metric with its null (`echo_null.py`) for any parent arm; controls = the same-stream unparented arm.\n- **Qualitative deliverable (Rohin wants to SEE the text):** at every probe checkpoint, 3 review states per life are sampled by seed and saved; decision packets show 6 per arm chosen by seed, never by hand.\n\n### 3.8 Analysis plan, pre-registration, reporting rules\n- Pre-registration file `research_notes/PREREG_v7_measurement.md` posted with a SEQ number and SHA-256 in COORDINATION: v1 before Stage A (arms, panels, cells, readouts), v2 before Stage B (n, thresholds fixed from pilot variance, pass marks). After v2 nothing moves; the tie-break for the record is the SEQ number/file order, not date strings (review M14).\n- Statistics: cell counts with the life as the unit; one-sided Fisher exact for binaries against the pre-registered baseline rate; cluster bootstrap over lives for means; slopes by least squares per life then compared across arms; no p-hacking of thresholds (all frozen in v2).\n- Reporting rules carried from the paper: no \"sealed\"/\"never seen\"; the child is Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (\"base\" = adapter OFF); anything measured on G is \"gate panel (selection)\"; two parents named separately; taught-vs-untaught statements always name the comparison arm and task family; regex counts labelled with audited precision; every parent arm labelled \"development-only children (contamination rule)\".\n- Pilot lives count toward Stage B n only if no code byte of their mechanism path changed between pilot and Stage B (source hashes bound at launch, as COORD:568 requires); otherwise they are development-only.\n\n### 3.9 Instrument fixes shipped with the v7 root (no change to running lives)\n- Forward `seed` in `VLLMBackend.__call__` (sleep THINK-calls are unseeded today, `model_backend.py:43-46`).\n- Freeze the wall-clock `alive` field in probe prompts (it varies per run, `state.py:34-38`).\n- `train_meta.json` records model id + HF snapshot hash + seed; launch scripts record source hashes of the mechanism path (`organism_v6/*.py`), panel JSON hashes, and flags (the migrate script currently drops `--probe-gate`/`--parent-url`; fixed).\n- Persist the shuffled curriculum list at launch and assert equality on resume (review minor 8); flag resumed lives in every table (minor 1).\n- Fix `classroom_round.py:360` to require ON ≥ PREV − tol (needed before any classroom source regeneration for a bootstrap).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Mechanisms built (each respects the rulings; build sizes are engineer-days, ED)\n\n| id | build | ruling respected | size |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| M1 | Probe cells (3)–(4): `run_probes_batch(memory=True)` renders `brief()` and reads RECALL from a read-only snapshot of the life ledger while writing to the probe ledger | \"always a baseline agent\"; M4 | 0.5 ED |\n| M2 | Panel builder: content hash, recipe/-Oz/-O3/best-of-256 references, headroom filter, two-sealer manifest → `panel_T_v2.json`, `panel_F_v1.json`, `panel_G_v1b.json`; `--report-panel`/`--gate-panel`/`--programs-json` flags in `run_life_v2` | split hygiene (held out by source); IDEAS:1380 | 0.5 ED + CPU hours on node |\n| M3 | TEXT arm at 1,024 episodes (exists: `--arm A`); add cell (3) probing for arm A | baseline ruling | 0.1 ED |\n| M4 | Provisional commit + confirm-or-revert; floor = max over confirmed probes; new rejection reason | gate design (SEQ-002 ASK) | 0.5–1 ED |\n| M5 | **v7 stream**: remove DONE from `_MARK`, `bootstrap.txt`, `_MD_MARK`, `classify`, ANCHORS; harness events `[SITUATION]` (program switch), `[SLEEP]`; markers PREDICT/ACT/NOTE/RECALL/**REVIEW** (a REVIEW ends a *state*, never the stream; the harness injects a review request 2 chunks before the situation budget ends if none was written); the review is pinned as `LAST REVIEW` at the HEAD of the next state; ledger rows carry `situation_id`, `state_id`, `phase`; `RECALL: state <id>` retrieves that state's plan + review verbatim (recurrent chain-of-thought); CLOCK stays as information; ritual/rehearsal metrics per state | no end token (IDEAS:1302); state-to-state (1351); recurrent CoT (1399); Codex: harness boundaries retained (COORD:674 item 3) | 3–4 ED |\n| M6 | **Branching**: `[BRANCH]` forks the current state's context into k = 4 seeded streams (branch id folded into the seed so streams do not collide), each runs to its own state end; a `[JOIN]` state shows the child the k reviews in randomized order and it writes one plan and commits one action; ledger tree (state_id, parent_state, branch_id). **Sleep rule (answer to Codex challenge a):** only the JOIN-committed transition (opening → synthesis → committed action → public outcome → review) is a write unit; branch rows are lossless ledger records tagged `branch_id`, never sleep targets, never replay. Matched-tokens variant: 4 × 4 chunks + JOIN (≈ 17 generations vs 16); generous variant: 4 × 16 + JOIN. Probes always run k = 1 (comparability); an as-deployed k = 4 probe on T at 512/1024 is reported separately | depth parallelism (1359); Codex separations (674 item 2); adjudicated write unit (scaled_think consensus) | 2–3 ED |\n| M7 | Near-duplicate collapse in the compiler: content-token Jaccard ≥ 0.8 → one row with a support count and provenance list; no recency window (cumulative CLS law kept); n_collapsed logged per sleep; exact dedup unchanged | compiler stays organizational (IDEAS 331–365, 1299); Codex dedup rule (674 item 8) | 0.5–1 ED |\n| M8 | Fading pattern parent: brief shown for 2 wakes (64 episodes) after each brief, then withdrawn; restored on relapse (ritual flagged again); REHEARSAL_TAIL only in the first two briefs; `SCANNER_VERSION` field; leak scan unchanged. Reconciles repetition (the child keeps rehearsing) with fading (the parent's text withdraws) | repetition ruling (1293); fading direction (1384); parent never enters the corpus | 0.5–1 ED |\n| M9 | Persistence split: wake curriculum of programs where recipe-once ≤ 0.02 and best-of-256 ≥ 0.15, content-hash-disjoint from P8/G/T/F; persistence score = HC on hard programs per visit + attempts after a non-improving outcome | classrooms are gyms, persistence first (1402) | 0.5 ED |\n| M10 | Provenance guard v0: birth manifest (base id + snapshot hash, adapter_config sha, bootstrap corpus sha + source-ledger shas + producing code versions, mechanism_version = hash of `organism_v6/*.py`, writer/compiler path, scanner version, prompt versions, exposure domains, parent-state hash, ancestor chain); `admit()` refuses launch on any missing field or quarantined ancestor; negative tests (copied dir without receipts → refuse; self-declared manifest → refuse). **Also fixes a real gap:** `launch_bootstrapped_life.sh` seeds `sleep_0000/adapter` but no `sleep_0000/corpus.json`, so the first sleep's clean-base retrain would discard the bootstrap; the bootstrap corpus must be carried as the prior corpus | Codex STOP (857); contamination rule (1346) | 1–2 ED |\n| M11 | Instruments: review-shape detector; actions-to-best; per-program revisit curves; 2×2 cell tables; slopes; binaries B1–B10 with Fisher; marker audit tool; qualitative sampler; `analysis_tables` v2 | scoping (1386); analysis rigor | 1.5 ED |\n| M12 | Finals runner: `--init-adapter` + `--init-corpus` + `--programs-json` + `--episodes 256` resuming a snapshot into a new life dir on F_train, probing F_test | second derivative (1384, 1390) | 0.5 ED |\n\n**Writer for the v7 root (pending Codex, ASK COORD:810).** Default for Stage A: keep the frozen v1 path (`compile_sleep` + bare-text trainer) for REF and V7 so that (a) the only difference between REF and V7-1 is the stream and (b) the existing R5 lives count as REF controls; run **REF-native** (v6 stream + `compile_native` dialect-matched rows + v2.1 chat-masked seeded trainer) as a fourth pilot arm so the writer question is decided by its harm/ritual/recipe-lock readouts rather than by fiat. Stage B rule (pre-registered): if REF-native has ≥ 2/4 harmful lives on T or P8 while R5/REF has 0 → Stage B stays on v1; if REF-native is harm-free and its ritual onset is not earlier → Stage B roots (REF, V7-*) switch to the native path and pilot V7 lives become development-only. Bootstrap arms require the corpus-carrying fix (M10) under either writer (`train_adapter.py` already reads the `a` field of `{q,a}` rows).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Arms and baselines (Stage A pilot n / Stage B n)\n\n| arm | stream | writer | gate | parent | wake curriculum | n A / B | primary comparator | falsifier (§5.1) |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **TEXT** | v6 loop, `--arm A` (no adapter; brief + NOTEs + RECALL) | none | none | none | standard 67 | 4 / 8 | cell (1) | baseline; F6 |\n| **R5** (existing) | v6 | v1 | G (no confirm) | none | standard | as launched (≥ 1) | — | writer bridge to the paper |\n| **REF** | v6 (DONE allowed) | v1 | G + provisional commit | none | standard | 4 / 8 | TEXT, cell (1) | F5, F6, F11 |\n| **REF-native** | v6 | compile_native + v2.1 | G + provisional | none | standard | 4 / 0–8 | REF | writer rule (§4) |\n| **V7-1** | v7 (no DONE, states, review pinned, RECALL by state) | as root | G + provisional | none | standard | 4 / 8 | REF | F1–F6, F11 |\n| **V7-4m** | v7 + k = 4 branches, matched tokens | as root | same | none | standard | 2 / 8 | V7-1 | F7 |\n| **V7-4g** | v7 + k = 4 × 16 + JOIN (generous) | as root | same | none | standard | 2 / 0 (8 only if 2/2 pilot lives pass F7) | V7-1 | F7 (total package) |\n| **V7-DD** | V7-1 + near-duplicate collapse | as root | same | none | standard | 0 / 8 | V7-1 | F8 |\n| **V7-PAR** | V7-1 + fading pattern parent (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct, named) | as root | same | 14B | standard | 0 / 8 | V7-1 | F9 (development-only children) |\n| **V7-PERSIST** | V7-1 | as root | same | none | persistence split | 0 / 8 | V7-1 | F10 |\n| **BOOT-S / BOOT-SHAM / BOOT-S+PAR** (conditional on M10 guard + Codex admission) | V7-1 born with a bootstrap adapter + carried corpus: structure-heavy metacognitive schooling vs form-matched non-metacognitive sham; V7-1 and V7-PAR are the \"none\" anchors | as root | same | none / 14B | standard | 0 / 8 each | V7-1, V7-PAR | F13 |\n| **FINALS** | age-1,024 children of REF, V7-1, V7-4m, V7-DD, V7-PAR deployed 256 episodes on F_train; + newborn V7-1 on F ×8; + TEXT on F ×8; optional parent-present deployments of V7-PAR children ×8 | as root | G | none (opt. 14B) | F_train | 8 per source arm | newborn, TEXT-on-F, cell (1) | F12 |\n\nAll arms: rank 8 from birth, budget 16, wake batch 8, sleep 32, seeds disjoint per arm (REF 800+, V7-1 900+, …), one life per GPU, parent servers on their own GPU (AWQ if 32B; TP = 2 bf16 hangs on A40s — 32B is not used here).\n\n### 5.1 Named falsifiers (cycle N, metric, margin, K seeds)\n\n| id | statement that may enter a report only if the falsifier is NOT met | cycle N | metric | margin | K |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| F1 | \"the v7 stream keeps changing behaviour across cycles\" | cycles 8–32 | median JS shift of first-ACT distribution | ≥ 0.15 in ≥ 6/8 V7-1 lives and ≤ 1/8 REF | 8 + 8 |\n| F2 | \"metacognitive markers survive the writes\" | cycles 16–32 | backtracks/episode (and contrast/episode) vs own cycles 2–4 | ratio ≥ 0.5 in ≥ 6/8; detector precision ≥ 0.8 | 8 |\n| F3 | \"the child writes review-shaped states\" | cycles 16–32 | fraction of states with ≥ 2 review elements | ≥ 0.5 in ≥ 6/8 V7-1 vs ≤ 1/8 REF (NOTE stream scored identically) | 8 + 8 |\n| F4 | \"the child improves on programs it revisits\" | visits 1→V over the life; cycles 1–32 | per-program revisit slope; wake best slope | slope > 0 with last−first ≥ +0.02 in ≥ 6/8; ≤ 1/8 REF | 8 + 8 |\n| F5 | \"the adapter transfers beyond recipe lock-in\" | age 128 → 1024 | cell (2)−(1) on T | ≥ +0.03 in ≥ 6/8 lives of the arm | 8 |\n| F6 | \"the weights carry gain the text does not\" | ages 512, 1024 | [(2)−(1)] − [(3)−(1)] on T | ≥ +0.02 at both ages in ≥ 6/8 REF (or V7-1) lives; if (3)−(1) ≥ (2)−(1) in ≥ 6/8, the weights-vs-tokens sentence is dropped for that arm | 8 |\n| F7 | \"k = 4 at matched tokens beats k = 1\" | situations over the life; F4/F5 | JOIN-committed score ≥ best single branch; F4 and F5 counts | ≥ 60% of situations AND V7-4m F4/F5 pass counts ≥ V7-1 + 2 lives | 8 + 8 |\n| F8 | \"near-duplicate collapse delays ritual and unlocks the recipe\" | ritual onset; all checkpoints | first ritual window; recipe-locked fraction of cell-(2) probes | onset ≥ 320 or never in ≥ 6/8 V7-DD vs ≤ 1/8 V7-1; locked fraction ≤ 0.25 vs 0.52–0.68 | 8 + 8 |\n| F9 | \"rehearsal persists after the parent's text withdraws\" | the 2 windows after each withdrawal | echo metric vs null on V7-1 lives; ritual windows after withdrawal | ≥ null + 0.10 in ≥ 6/8 V7-PAR lives; ritual-window rate ≤ ½ of V7-1 | 8 + 8 |\n| F10 | \"the persistence gym instils persistence\" | visits ≥ 8; cycles 16–32 | HC on hard programs (visit ≥ 8 − visit 1); backtracks/episode | ≥ +0.10 in ≥ 6/8; ≥ 0.5/episode in ≥ 6/8 | 8 |\n| F11 | \"the gate keeps harm rare on a panel it never selected on\" | all checkpoints (≥ 14 per life) | lives with ≥ 1 pair (2)−(1) < −0.03 on T | ≤ 2/24 gated root-writer lives (≥ 5/24 → gate redesign) | 24 |\n| F12 | \"the developed child learns a fresh family faster\" (programme target) | deployment checkpoints 64–256 | slope of cell (4) [and (2)] on F_test | developed V7-1 slope > newborn by ≥ 0.01/checkpoint AND > TEXT-on-F by ≥ 0.01 in ≥ 6/8 children; entry level reported separately; null = \"not yet\" | 8 + 8 + 8 |\n| F13 | \"the bootstrap installs the reflecting habit\" (conditional) | first 64 episodes, no parent; ritual onset | review detector ≥ 1 per state in ≥ 50% of states; onset | ≥ 6/8 BOOT-S vs ≤ 1/8 sham; onset ≥ 320 in ≥ 6/8 vs ≤ 2/8 | 8 + 8 |\n\nEvery falsifier is also a **falsifier against the baselines**: F4/F5/F6 must hold relative to TEXT (cell 3) and cell (1); a v7 sentence that passes against REF but not against TEXT is not written.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Gyms and traits\n\n**The world:** CompilerGym llvm-v0, IR instruction-count reduction (deterministic, hidden dynamics, not memorized per program). Splits: standard curriculum (67 cbench/chstone/mibench programs), persistence split (M9), P8, G, T, F — all content-hash-disjoint except P8's known same-source siblings (disclosed). Sanity check of scale: the ledger per life is ~300 MB with stored prompt renders; a 16-program panel × 4 cells × 16 chunks = 1,024 generations per full checkpoint, well inside a GPU-hour.\n\n**The classroom domain (target-blind):** the rule game (hidden rule over integer triples; 10 rules, held-out instances) remains the only admissible source for bootstrap material and for any future parenting classroom; compound/sequence rules (B5) are a later build and not part of this set. Rule-game exam noise (SD 0.030–0.044 at 40 episodes) makes it unsuitable as a measurement panel here.\n\n**Traits → instruments (what \"instil\" means as a number):**\n\n| trait | instrument (per episode/state, per cycle) | baseline today |\n|---|---|---|\n| persistence | attempts after a non-improving outcome (backtracks); actions per situation; HC on hard programs across visits | backtracks 0.53 → 0.0055 over life |\n| reflection | review-shape elements per state; references to earlier states; states per situation | none (no states in v6) |\n| exploration | distinct ACTs per situation; JS shift between cycles; recipe-locked fraction | JS median 0.048; locked 52–68% |\n| self-evaluation | self-eval statements whose polarity matches the outcome; |PREDICT − score| (calibration) by cycle | PREDICT SD flattens (ritual flag) |\n| re-planning after failure | plan revision after a wrong PREDICT (classify: PREDICT … WRONG/REVIS) | ≈ 0 late |\n\nGyms give environmental feedback; the parent (V7-PAR only) is one feedback channel, a critic that never gives answers (process-only by prompt and leak-scanned; scanner version logged), target-blind on T and F by construction (it only ever sees curriculum-episode text).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Compute: per-life cost, budget, quota arithmetic\n\n**Per-life model** (wake 1,024 episodes ≈ 40 h at the measured life rate; 32 sleeps + engine reloads ≈ 6 h; probe episodes ≈ 80/h in batches of 8–16): probe episodes per life = P8 256 + T 256 + memory cells 128 + G 384 (gated) = 1,024.\n\n| life type | GPU-h |\n|---|---|\n| TEXT (arm A; no training, no G) | ≈ 50 |\n| REF / REF-native (gated, v6 stream) | ≈ 59 |\n| V7-1 / V7-DD / V7-PAR / V7-PERSIST (no-DONE fills the budget: +25% wake) | ≈ 69 |\n| V7-4m (matched tokens) | ≈ 73 |\n| V7-4g (generous, 4× wake generation) | ≈ 189 |\n| finals deployment (256 episodes + 8 sleeps + 4 F_test checkpoints) | ≈ 17 |\n\n| stage | lives | GPU-h |\n|---|---|---|\n| Stage 0 (node 2 before 09-21; CPU panel precheck; brief proxy if not yet run) | — | ~10 |\n| Stage A pilot: TEXT 4, REF 4, REF-native 4, V7-1 4, V7-4m 2, V7-4g 2 (+ smoke) | 20 | ~1,530 |\n| Stage B: TEXT +4, REF +4, V7-1 +4, V7-4m +6, V7-DD 8, V7-PAR 8 (+ parent server 12 days), V7-PERSIST 8 | 42 | ~3,100 |\n| Finals: 5 × 8 deployments + newborn 8 + TEXT-on-F 8 (+ optional parent-present 8 + server) | 56–64 | ~1,250 |\n| Instrument runs, detector audits, hinge replications (2 lives), 10% contingency | — | ~600 |\n| **Core total** | | **≈ 6,300–6,500 GPU-h** |\n| Conditional bootstrap: classroom source regeneration under versioned code (~260), 3 arms × 8 lives (~1,850 incl. server), their finals (~410) | 24 + 24 | **≈ 2,500** |\n| **Envelope** | | **≈ 8,800–9,000 GPU-h** |\n\nOn 16 A40s the core is ~16 days at full occupancy, ~24 days with wave imbalance; the conditional bootstrap adds ~7 days. This fits 09-27 → 11-05.\n\n**Quota arithmetic (assumption to verify with Colossus: 1 unit = 1 node-lease-hour; 1,440 per trailing 30 days; future-dated bookings count at creation).** On 09-26 the trailing window still holds node 1 through 09-14 (≈ 432 h) and node 2 through 09-21 (≈ 600 h) ≈ 1,032 h, leaving ≈ 408 h: one node for ~17 days, not two. By 10-08 the window frees to ≈ 720 h. **Action on 09-19:** request a quota increase to ≥ 2,200 h/30 days via Colossus Support and book two nodes from 09-26 for 35 days. **Fallback without the increase:** one node from 09-27 (Stage A runs at n = 2–4 per arm over two waves), second node from 10-08; Stage B and finals slip about one week (numbers freeze 11-27). Node provisioning (vLLM stack via `gpu/v2_bootstrap.sh`, CompilerGym venv relayed as a single tarball through the laptop, environment capture, HF snapshot pin) is budgeted at one day per node.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Calendar (day by day from 2026-09-19; Sep 19 is a Saturday)\n\n**Stage 0 — node 2 alive, GPUs on the paper's optional replication; CPU work only**\n- **Sat 09-19.** File the Colossus quota-increase and two-node lease request (from 09-26, 35 days). On node 2 (CPU): check which large datasets install (poj104-v1, github-v0, linux-v0, anghabench-v1); start the panel precheck (content hash, recipe-once, -Oz/-O3, best-of-256 random 3–8-pass sequences per candidate program; parallel across cores). Compute the per-program revisit-curve baseline and the per-life recipe-lock (B7) table for all finished lives from ledgers. Post `PREREG_v7_measurement.md` v0 (this design) with a SEQ number.\n- **Sun 09-20.** Paper numbers freeze (evening) — nothing here touches it. Precheck finishes; T (16), F_train (32), F_test (12) candidate lists produced; G decision on the three tensorflow programs (§9 D1c); two-sealer agreement (Fable + Codex) recorded with manifest hashes; panel JSONs committed. Baseline table for B1–B7 finalized from disk.\n- **Mon 09-21.** Node 2 lease ends: confirm `~/v6_out` mirror to the laptop (SHA-256) and the environment capture. Builds begin (no GPU needed): M1 probe cells, M3 arm-A cell (3), M4 provisional commit, instrument fixes (§3.9).\n\n**Build week (paper is being finished in parallel; nothing GPU)**\n- **Tue 09-22 – Thu 09-24.** M5 v7 stream with unit tests on recorded ledgers (marker parsing, situation/state boundaries, review pinning, RECALL by state id, behaviour canary redefinition); M11 detectors (review shape, actions-to-best, revisit curves, 2×2 tables, binaries, Fisher); M6 branching core (fork/JOIN, seeds, ledger tree, committed-transition compile). ASKs to Codex posted in one entry: gate definition (provisional + confirmed floor), split hygiene ratification, dedup protocol, branch compile rule, probe-under-k, writer for the root.\n- **Fri 09-25.** ICLR paper submitted. Pre-registration **v1** frozen (arms, panels, cells, readouts, Stage A n) → SEQ entry with SHA-256.\n- **Sat 09-26 – Sun 09-27.** New nodes online (assumed): provisioning + smoke tests (2 h each): v7 stream 64-episode smoke on 2 GPUs (no-DONE runs to budget; states/reviews parsed; canary sane), TEXT smoke, memory-cell probe smoke (probe rows never enter the life ledger — asserted), provisional-commit smoke (forced revert path), branching smoke (k = 4 seeds disjoint; JOIN commits exactly one action). Run-order discipline: no life launches until every smoke trace is read.\n\n**Stage A — pilot (variance components, plumbing)**\n- **Mon 09-28.** Launch 16 lives: TEXT ×4, REF ×4, REF-native ×4, V7-1 ×4 → wait, 16 GPUs: TEXT 4 + REF 4 + V7-1 4 + REF-native 2 + V7-4m 2 (day 1); REF-native +2 and V7-4g ×2 launch on 09-30/10-01 as TEXT lives finish. (Pilot n as in §5: TEXT 4, REF 4, REF-native 4, V7-1 4, V7-4m 2, V7-4g 2.)\n- **Tue 09-29 – Fri 10-02.** Lives run (TEXT ≈ 50 h, REF ≈ 59 h, V7-1 ≈ 69 h, V7-4m ≈ 73 h; V7-4g ≈ 8 days). Meanwhile: M7 dedup, M8 fading parent, M9 persistence split, M10 guard v0 + negative tests, M12 finals runner; marker audit rubric written; daemon polls both nodes.\n- **Sat 10-03 – Sun 10-04.** Stage A analysis from disk: between-life SD on T of (2)−(1), of slopes, of HC; B1–B10 counts per arm at n = 4; detector precision audit (50 chunks/arm); rejection logs; qualitative samples (6 per arm by seed). **Decision D-A** (§9). Pre-registration **v2**: Stage B n, thresholds for B9/B10 fixed from pilot variance, writer rule applied → SEQ entry with hash. Rohin's authorization of v2 (his decision iii).\n\n**Stage B — confirmatory arms**\n- **Mon 10-05.** Wave 1 on the 12–14 free GPUs: TEXT +4, REF +4, V7-1 +4, V7-DD ×2.\n- **Wed 10-07 – Thu 10-08.** Wave 2 as GPUs free: V7-4m +6, V7-DD +6; parent server (14B) up on one GPU; V7-PAR ×3.\n- **Sat 10-10 – Sun 10-11.** Wave 3: V7-PAR +5, V7-PERSIST ×8 (some start 10-12). V7-4g pilot lives finish ~10-06 → total-package readout at n = 2 (descriptive; decides whether V7-4g gets 8 lives in the slack of 10-15–10-18).\n- **Mon 10-12 – Fri 10-16.** Waves finish; interim tables (pre-registered readouts only; no threshold changes). Guard v0 target: negative tests pass by 10-10 → Codex admission decision on regenerating a target-blind bootstrap source under versioned classroom code (`classroom_round.py:360` fixed first).\n- **Sat 10-17 – Sun 10-18.** Slack: hinge replications (any arm at 5/8), V7-4g ×8 only if 2/2 pilot lives passed F7.\n- **Mon 10-19 – Fri 10-23.** **Finals**: deploy age-1,024 children (REF, V7-1, V7-4m, V7-DD, V7-PAR; 8 each) on F_train for 256 episodes; newborn V7-1 on F ×8; TEXT-on-F ×8; optional parent-present deployments of V7-PAR children ×8. ~17 h each; ~3 days on 16 GPUs. Conditional (if admitted): classroom source regeneration 10-12–10-15 on 2–3 GPUs + parent server; bootstrap adapters trained and ritual-on-corpus measured 10-16; BOOT-S/SHAM/S+PAR ×8 launched as finals GPUs free, 10-22 → ~10-30.\n- **Sat 10-24 – Fri 10-30.** Finals analysis (entry level vs slope per child); BOOT arms running; T re-probes of every held adapter (2 reps) for the per-life tables.\n- **Sat 10-31 – Thu 11-05.** BOOT finals (24 deployments); full analysis freeze v1: life-level tables, Fisher tests, slopes, 2×2 cells, audited marker counts, qualitative samples; decision packet to Rohin with recommendation + reason codes.\n- **Fri 11-06 – Wed 11-11.** Codex adversarial number-to-source audit against the pre-registration hash; auditor's brief (five most attackable claims); fixes limited to arithmetic and labelling.\n- **Thu 11-12 – Thu 11-19.** Replications for any claim that hinges on ≤ 2 lives (2 extra lives per hinge arm); leases released as GPUs go idle.\n- **Fri 11-20.** **Numbers freeze.** Rohin writes; venues: ICML 2027 (~late Jan; verify CFP), ICLR 2027 workshops (~early Feb), CoLM 2027 (~late Mar) as the safe harbor.\n\nTotal: 63 days (09-19 → 11-20).\n\n---\n\n## 9. Decision points that pending data resolve, and how each outcome changes this design\n\n**D1. Disjoint-panel re-probe (F1b; running on both nodes; first cell R2 seed2 −0.005/−0.008 vs base 0.257).**\n- (a) If ≥ 80% of gated finals (R3/R4) are ≥ base − 0.03 on panel v1 → the G-gate design is kept as is (plus provisional commit); the abstract's gated-safety sentence exists and F11's pooled ≤ 2/24 becomes the confirmatory bar. If < 80% → the gate's selection does not transfer; Stage A adds a second gate probe (k = 2 probes per candidate) and F11 becomes the first readout of Stage A before any v7 interpretation.\n- (b) If every re-probed final sits within ±0.01 of base (recipe lock-in with zero transfer, as the first cell suggests) → B9's expected baseline is 0/8 and the T panel's headroom rule (≥ 0.10 search headroom) is confirmed as mandatory; F5 is the only place a v7 gain can appear, and the P8 continuity probes drop to every 128 to buy budget for T.\n- (c) The three tensorflow programs: dropped from G if their per-adapter ON−OFF spread across the ~30 re-probed adapters is < 0.005 (uninformative), else kept.\n- (d) If the re-probe shows between-life SD of (2)−(1) on panel v1 ≤ 0.01 → n = 8 resolves a 0.015 transfer difference (a mean test becomes viable on T and is added to F5 as a secondary, pre-registered in v2).\n\n**D2. Text-memory brief-only proxy (M4; `gpu/brief_baseline.sh`, ~5–10 GPU-h, queued).**\n- If OFF+brief ≥ ON on P8 in ≥ 5/9 R2 lives → the paper drops the weights-vs-tokens frame; here, cell (3) is promoted to the primary comparator in every falsifier (already the case for F6) and the TEXT arm is raised to n = 12 at the expense of V7-PERSIST (drops to 4).\n- If ON > OFF+brief by ≥ 0.02 in ≥ 6/9 → the weights effect on P8 is real; F6 keeps its ≥ 0.02 margin and cell (2)−(3) becomes a headline cell in the decision packet.\n- If not run before 09-21 → nothing changes here (the TEXT arm and cells 3–4 supersede the proxy), but the paper carries \"no text-memory arm was run\".\n\n**D3. R5 (gate on disjoint panel, seed 700+; first life launched at SEQ-006; zero probe pairs then).**\n- If R5's P8 harm count and life mean match R3's post-selection numbers (0 harmful; +0.02–0.05) → R5 is the REF definition minus provisional commit, and R5 lives count toward REF's writer-bridge cell.\n- If R5 shows ≥ 1 harmful pair on P8 with the disjoint gate → the gate did not generalize; provisional commit becomes mandatory in REF and Stage A adds k = 2 gate probes; F11's bar tightens to 0/24.\n- Codex's ratification of \"floor = max over confirmed probes; commit provisional until confirmed\" decides whether REF's gate is the freeze recipe or a labelled variant.\n\n**D4. R4 (gate + parent v3, seeds 600–606).**\n- If ≥ 4/7 R4 lives at 1,024 keep contrast statements ≥ 0.5 × early through cycle 32 (node-1 R4 rose 5.2 → 10.0 at ≤ 380 episodes) → V7-PAR keeps priority and its fading schedule uses R4's brief cadence as the \"before withdrawal\" reference; F9 is expected to be reachable.\n- If R4's markers fall to the floor like every other arm and taught-vs-untaught does not separate on efficiency → V7-PAR is the first arm cut when GPUs are short (n → 4), and the parent's fading schedule is tested on 2 lives only as a plumbing check.\n- If the disjoint re-probe shows R4 finals below base → \"parenting keeps the write from collapsing\" is withdrawn entirely and V7-PAR is reported as an intervention on development children with F9 only (no safety sentence).\n\n**D5. Panel precheck (Stage 0).**\n- If ≥ 16 programs in one installed source meet the headroom rule → T as designed. If only across two sources → T spans two sources, reported per source. If fewer than 16 anywhere → CompilerGym cannot host the situation-dependent test; F5/F12 are re-scoped to HC on the persistence split and the finals family F moves to compound-rule games (a build, +2 ED) — flagged as the largest design risk.\n\n**D6. Codex ratifications (one entry, requested 09-22).** Gate definition (D3); split hygiene (the gym's training split as a parenting environment — needed only for V7-PAR here; already satisfied since the parent sees curriculum text only); dedup protocol (M7 thresholds; if Codex requires proposition-level equivalence rather than Jaccard, M7 becomes exact-dedup-plus-count-cap and F8's expected effect shrinks); branch compile rule (M6's committed-transition write unit; if Codex rules JOIN syntheses ineligible, V7-4m writes only the committed action/outcome/review and the JOIN text is context only); probe-under-k (k = 1 primary; if Codex requires k = train, a second probe budget is added and comparability with REF is stated as limited); writer for the root (§4 rule).\n\n**D7. Provenance guard (M10) and Codex admission.** If the guard passes its negative tests by 10-10 and Codex admits a regenerated target-blind source → the conditional bootstrap arms launch 10-22. If not by 10-16 → bootstrap arms move to the next experiment set; this set reports the bootstrap only as a pre-registered protocol. Under no outcome do v1/v2 (QUARANTINE_TASK_EXPOSED) or v3 (DEV_UNVERIFIED_PROVENANCE) adapters birth a clean or parented child.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Risks and mitigations\n\n| risk | why it is real | mitigation |\n|---|---|---|\n| CompilerGym cannot reward situation-dependent choice | on panel v1 the recipe already ≈ -Oz (gaps 0.00–0.07) | headroom rule with a search reference; D5 fallback to compound-rule games |\n| Quota blocks a second node until ~10-08 | rolling 30-day window nearly saturated by the current nodes | request increase 09-19; fallback schedule (+1 week) |\n| No-DONE stream degenerates (idle loops, zero actions) | the brevity gate presupposed early ending; base fills 13/16 chunks | redefined behaviour canary (actions per situation, distinct ACTs); harness review request; smoke before launch |\n| Regex marker counts are noisy | SEQ-005 caveat (\"instead\"/\"correct\") | precision audit ≥ 0.8 or counts are demoted to \"detector-flagged\" |\n| n = 8 underpowered for mean effects | MDE 0.048 on life means; floor from confirmed probes |\n| k = 4 generous variant is expensive (~190 GPU-h/life) | 4× wake generation | pilot n = 2; Stage B only if 2/2 pass F7(a) |\n| Bootstrap arms blocked or discarded at the first sleep | Codex STOP; `launch_bootstrapped_life.sh` carries no corpus | guard v0 + corpus carry-over (M10); conditional launch |\n| Parent leaks answers or text enters the corpus | scanner porous (5/32 residual in the rule game) | process-only prompt, leak scan with version, quote-back exemption; parent text never in the corpus; V7-PAR labelled development-only |\n| Writer confound between REF and the paper's lives | REF-native changes compiler and trainer | R5 (v1) lives as the bridge; pilot rule decides the root writer mechanically |\n| Node provisioning failures (CompilerGym venv relay corruption) | measured on 09-07 | single-tarball relay, one day per node budgeted, smoke tests before any life |\n| Pre-registration drift | thresholds tempting to move after seeing data | v2 hash posted before Stage B; changes only via a new SEQ entry labelled post hoc |\n| COORD record is not chronological | dates out of order (review M14) | every artifact cites SEQ numbers and file hashes |\n\n---\n\n## 11. Open items for Codex and Rohin\n\n**Codex (ratify before 09-25):** gate definition (provisional + confirmed floor); split hygiene; dedup protocol (M7); branch compile rule (M6 write unit); probe-under-k; writer for the v7 root; the two-sealer panel manifests (T, F, G v1b); the pre-registration v1 text; guard schema (M10) and negative tests; whether a regenerated rule-game classroom source under versioned code is admissible as the Stage-1 corpus.\n\n**Rohin (three decisions):** (i) confirm the scientific object (§0); (ii) authorize the two-node lease + quota request and the ~6,500 + 2,500 GPU-h envelope; (iii) sign the pre-registration v2 hash on 10-04 (after that, thresholds are frozen). Also his reconciling sentence on repetition vs fading (this design assumes: the child keeps rehearsing while the parent's text withdraws) and on \"sleep converges branches\" vs \"branches are not sleep targets\" (this design assumes Codex's adjudicated write unit: only the JOIN-committed transition writes).\n\n**Answers to Codex's three challenges (so directions 2 and 5 are unblocked in the right order):** (a) committed-branch-only SLEEP — yes; branch drafts are lossless ledger records with `branch_id`, replay-ineligible; (b) conversational opinions — not built in this set; they would need the separate support class Codex describes, and no instrument here measures them; (c) sibling-fork vs pooled-clone — this set runs no pooled clones; every life is an independent student; the ledger gains `source_id`/`clone_id` so the clone merge (a speed mechanism, readout = first derivative per wall-hour) can be measured once a positive first derivative exists to speed up.\n\n---\n\n## Appendix A — baseline numbers used and where they come from\n- Life table, SDs, per-program, ritual-in-weights: COORDINATION SEQ-001 (per-life means R2: +0.037, +0.050, −0.000, +0.045, +0.022, −0.051, −0.005, +0.050, +0.028; RP +0.038, +0.016, +0.011; harmful lives R2 4/9, RP 1/3, R3 0/6 post-selection).\n- Heavy tail and floor decay: SEQ-002. The curriculum: gyms, traits, splits\n\nAll classrooms are gyms (IDEAS:1402). Each gym must have hidden dynamics the model cannot have memorized, a verifiable score, procedurally generated instances, a source-disjoint exam, and a payoff that arrives only after many changing attempts. Under the v7 stream the child never emits DONE; the **harness seals a situation** (quiz submitted, lock opened, budget reached) and injects `[SITUATION]` lines; 2 white-space chunks follow each seal for the REVIEW state before the next situation (Codex item 3, COORD:701-707: harness boundaries retained, clock is information).\n\n### G1. RuleGame-v2 (target-blind; extends `rulegame.py`) — traits: reflection, self-evaluation, exploration\n- **Tiers.** T1: the existing 10 atomic rules (kept so old classroom exams stay comparable). T2: compound rules = two atomic predicates joined by AND / OR / A-AND-NOT-B (10·9·3 = 270 families). T3: sequence rules with hidden state (answer depends on the previous TRY, e.g. \"sum increased vs your last probe\"). T4: landscape boxes — the box returns a number f(a,b,c) from a seeded composition of primitives; the quiz asks for values at held-out points and the argmax (exploration before commitment).\n- **Actions.** `TRY a,b,c` (feedback True/False or value). `QUIZ` answers with per-item confidence (T/F plus 0.5–1.0). **One QUIZ per box; the quiz is INVALID before 4 probes** (gym rule = environmental feedback, not parent text). After the quiz the harness seals the box: `[SITUATION] box sealed; quiz score 0.83; nothing to act on until the next box` → review white space.\n- **Score.** 1 − Brier over quiz items (proper scoring rule; rewards calibrated self-evaluation, punishes confident wrong answers).\n- **Splits.** Training instances by eid namespace; **exam = held-out families** (T2 pairs never seen in training, T3 rules never seen) plus held-out instances of seen families — a within-gym near/far transfer split (62_:102-121 warning).\n- **Exam.** 40 eids, budget 12, seeded (rule-game noise SD 0.030–0.044 at 40 eids, EVIDENCE:8 → margin 0.06). Noise band re-measured for v2 with 8 seeded frozen-model reps before any number is read (`rulegame_noise.py` pattern).\n\n### G2. LockGym (target-blind, new ~150 lines) — traits: persistence, re-planning after failure\n- **Hidden code** of length 4 over symbols 1–6 (T1), length 5 over 1–8 (T2), T3 = one position's feedback always hidden (hidden dynamics). `GUESS s1..sL` → `[OUTCOME] exact=k partial=m`. Payoff only after many changing attempts; each feedback contradicts hypotheses (re-planning).\n- **Score.** max over attempts of exact/L; 1.0 when opened (progress is monotone in continued, changing effort; early quitting cannot score). On open: `[SITUATION] lock opened after 7 attempts` → review.\n- **Exam.** 24 codes from a held-out seed range, T1+T2 (T2 held out entirely in the far-transfer variant). Noise band: 8 seeded frozen-model reps first.\n\n### G3. Low-headroom rule in §11.\n- **Fallback if github-v0 / linux-v0 / poj104-v1 cannot be installed on the nodes:** novel family = opencv-v0 (442 programs; train/held-out by identifier + IR hash), report = tensorflow-v0 programs with h ≥ 0.10, and the gate panel is rebuilt from npb-v0 + blas-v0 only so no source is shared between gate and report.\n- **Every panel JSON records** recipe score, -Oz, -O3 per program (SEQ-003 style) and the frozen model ×3 seeded reps before any adapter is probed.\n\n### Trait → gym → instrument map\n| Trait | Gym(s) | Per-situation instrument (averaged per cycle, ON vs OFF, taught vs untaught) | Today's baseline |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | G2, G3, Stage-3 gym | distinct ACTs after the last improvement; backtracks (ACT change after a non-improving outcome); attempts per situation vs frozen model; LockGym open rate | backtracks 0.33–0.89 early → 0.00–0.03 late |\n| Re-planning after failure | G2, G1-T3 | P(next ACT changes \\| feedback contradicts the stated hypothesis) | not measured |\n| Reflection | all (REVIEW state) | review-marker rate (went wrong / next time / generalises / scope / did well); review carry-over (next situation's first NOTE references the review AND the first ACT changes when the review named a change); 5 sampled reviews per cycle stored for reading (Rohin's deliverable is the text, IDEAS:1352) | contrast phrases 4.7–6.7 early → 0.00–0.09 late |\n| Exploration | G1-T4, G3 | distinct probes before the quiz; strategy switches (distinct ACTs); coverage entropy on landscape boxes | switches 2.0–3.2 → 1.0–2.7 |\n| Self-evaluation | G1 (confidence quiz), all (PREDICT) | Brier of PREDICT vs outcome; quiz Brier; named-belief revision after surprise (wrong PREDICT → NOTE naming the belief within 2 chunks → changed ACT) | PREDICT SD < 0.05 = ritual flag |\n| Behaviour change (day-one gate) | all | Jensen–Shannon shift of the first-ACT distribution between consecutive cycles, per clone | 0.02–0.13 |\n| Ritual | all | frozen 09-09 definition per clone per 32-situation window (both definitions reported, review M6) | onset 160–224 in 8/9 lives |\n\nMarker regexes are noisy (SEQ-005: \"instead\"/\"correct\" over-count); counts are diagnostics, success is a changed committed action and public outcome (Codex item 5, COORD:720-729).\n\n---\n\n## 4. The critic and environmental feedback\n\n**Paper wording stays:** \"process-only by prompt and leak-scanned\" (review M8), never \"never gives answers\" as a proven property.\n\n- **Model.** Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct on one A40 per node (`launch_parent_server.sh`); AWQ-32B is an optional second parent, **named separately in every table, never reported as a size effect** (review M6). Weights never learn; the parent improves only at the ledger/playbook layer (IDEAS:144-169).\n- **Trigger and input.** At every sealed situation in a classroom clone, the critic receives: the child's REVIEW state (last review NOTE), the last 8 tail chunks, the harness admission record (score, attempts, whether the situation improved on the clone's running mean for that gym), and the child's playbook. Output ≤ 100 words: one process observation + one question. ~160 calls per cycle per child (32 + 64 + 64 situations), batched through a thread pool as in `classroom_round.py:210`.\n- **Contingent ladder (PARENTING_MENU #1; harness-computed from the admission record).** Level 0 after an improving situation: one question only. Level 1 after one non-improving situation: name the process-mistake category. Level 2 after two consecutive: state the correction plainly with the reason — still process, never the rule, code, or pass.\n- **Fading and relapse (Codex COORD:630-636; PARENTING_MENU #13; reconciles RULED repetition with DIRECTION fading).** The critic's text is shown in the HEAD for the next situation only. The critic goes silent for a gym once the clone's admit rate over the last 16 situations ≥ 0.6 **and** its review-marker rate ≥ 2× the frozen model's; it is restored on relapse (admit rate < 0.4, or a ritual flag). **The child's own rehearsal continues without the parent's text:** the child's boot asks it, once, to open each situation with a NOTE restating in its own words what it currently believes about this kind of situation. Parent text fades; child repetition stays. Uptake is measured as unprompted enactment that changes the next action and improves a public outcome and survives context reset — first-NOTE overlap remains an echo diagnostic only (SEQ-003: 0.454 vs 0.354 controls).\n- **Pattern parent (sleep-time ritual brief).** Kept as a second channel only if R4 resolves in its favour (§11); if kept, it obeys the same fading rule (brief shown for 2 wakes, withdrawn, restored on a persisting ritual flag).\n- **Leak scan.** `leak_scan` with per-gym `extra_terms`: rule descriptors (all tiers, including compound paraphrases), lock-code digit sequences, the full LLVM pass list (not six passes); quote-back exemption retained for the child's own recipe words; `SCANNER_VERSION` written into every parent row; FALLBACK on hits with fallback rate reported per child. A CPU leak audit of every delivered utterance per lineage is a standing job (review M8).\n- **Thinker/compiler line.** Parent rows are `kind=\"parent\"` in the child's parent ledger and are **never compiled**. The child's immediate restatement (`kind=\"restate\"`) is ledgered and **not compiled**; only the child's own text inside situations with a committed ACT is eligible (Codex COORD:731-740: an opinion enters SLEEP only after it is instantiated in a later committed trace).\n- **Principal (speed parallelism, IDEAS:1362).** One `ParentLedger` per child (not per clone); every row carries `clone_id`, `env_id`, `situation_id`; the bounded playbook is rewritten once per merged sleep from all clones' rows.\n- **Target-blindness.** The parent never sees exam instances, panel programs, or Stage-3 programs; at Stage 3 there is no parent (deployment is parent-absent, IDEAS:15-31).\n\n**Environmental feedback (harness, not parent):** `[OUTCOME]` lines; `[SITUATION]` seal lines with the public result; CLOCK \"chunks since last progress\"; the quiz-before-4-probes INVALID rule; LockGym's monotone partial credit. No -Oz hint, no headroom hint.\n\n---\n\n## 5. One child, many clones, merged at sleep (speed parallelism)\n\nCodex's boundary (COORD:709-718; shared brief §B): shallow synchronized fan-out, no local weight updates, pool the set-union of admitted evidence, **merge corpora never weights**, one cumulative write, redistribute. Pooled-clone childhood is labelled as its own condition (\"merged-3-clone childhood\"), not \"one life\".\n\n- **Topology per child.** 3 clone processes on one node (G1, G2, G3), one child directory, one parent server and one evaluator GPU shared by the 2 children on that node (3+3+1+1 = 8 GPUs). Each clone plays one wake per cycle: G3 32 situations (CompilerGym, ~1.8 h at ~18 situations/h under no-DONE), G1 and G2 64 situations each (cheaper gyms; sized so all clones finish within ~1.8 h). Cycle = 160 situations per child; 16 cycles = 2,560 situations per child (≈ 2.5 lives of experience in ~41 h wall-clock).\n- **Barrier.** Each clone writes `READY_{cycle}` after its wake and releases its vLLM engine (`close_backend`); the coordinator waits for all `READY`, compiles, trains on one freed clone GPU, gates, commits, writes `GO_{cycle}`; clones reload `latest_adapter` (the reload path already exists, run_life_v2:334) and resume. Idempotent markers everywhere; a clone crash resumes from its last wake marker.\n- **Compile (organizational only).** Rows in the `compile_native` shape: `q` = the stored prompt render, `a` = the child's own chunk, from thought rows with `win` or `had_note` **inside situations that contain ≥ 1 committed ACT with a public outcome**, plus each such situation's REVIEW chunk. Provenance per row: `child_id, clone_id, env_id, situation_id, state_id, evidence_ids (act rows), cycle, mechanism_version`. Exact dedup on `a[:160]`; **near-duplicate collapse only under Codex's equivalence** (same env, same ACT set, same outcome class, content-token Jaccard ≥ 0.8): collapse to one training view, keep `support` count and all provenance (COORD:750-756). No parent rows, no restate rows, no exam rows (exam ledgers live in the evaluator's directory). Cumulative corpus (CLS law) with env strata ≥ 0.2 each via `stratified_mix`, so no gym's volume swamps another's (IDEAS:208-213). **Training-view cap 4,000 rows per write** by stratified subsample (all evidence retained in the corpus store; replay exposure is a controlled dose, not erased evidence). `n_collapsed`, rows per env, chars per row logged per sleep (chunk density logged, never claimed; Codex item 9).\n- **Write.** v2.2 trainer (chat-masked, response-only loss, lr 3e-5, 2 epochs, rank 8, bsz 4, MAXLEN 1024 left-truncated prompt, `seed = crc32(child_id, cycle)`), from the clean base over the cumulative capped corpus. `train_meta.json` records model snapshot hash, seed, corpus sha, mechanism version (review M10). **Why v2.2 for this root:** rows are dialect-matched and provenance-tagged (needed for the merge and the split-hygiene ablation), response-only loss is Codex's writer prerequisite (64_:161-167), and `absorption_probe` then matches the trainer (review M9). Cost check: 4,000 rows × 2 epochs / bsz 4 = 2,000 steps ≈ 35 min at ~1 s/step — measured in the pilot; if > 45 min, the cap drops to 3,000 or the v1 bare-text trainer (≈ 4× cheaper per row) is chosen for the whole lineage **before** the confirmatory launch, never mid-lineage. SVD subspace init (TMEM) is logged with the trigger \"write time is the bottleneck\", not built.\n- **Gate (anything called the freeze recipe must judge scores and behaviour, IDEAS:1289-1291; SEQ-002).**\n  1. Format canary in each gym's real context: parseable-ACT rate ≥ 0.5 × OFF on 4 situations per gym.\n  2. Score gate on panel v1 (12 programs, budget 16, seeded): candidate ≥ max(gate_base, best **confirmed** committed gate probe) − 0.02 (no floor decay).\n  3. Behaviour canary redefined under no-DONE (review minor 11): on the gate probe, ACTs per situation ≥ 0.5 × OFF's and distinct ACTs per situation ≥ 0.5 × OFF's.\n  4. Trait-gym collapse canary: 8 held-out G1 and 8 held-out G2 instances ON vs PREV, HARM_TOL 0.10 (catches catastrophic collapse only; 8-eid noise SD ≈ 0.07). Fixes the classroom defect that ignored PREV (`classroom_round.py:357-360`).\n  5. **Provisional commit:** `DONE_PROVISIONAL` mounts; confirmed at the next cycle's gate probe (ON ≥ floor − tol) → `DONE`; else `REJECTED_UNCONFIRMED` and clones reload the previous `DONE` (wake rows generated under a reverted adapter remain experience). Pending Codex ratification of the SEQ-002 ASK; default ON for v7c.\n  Rejection reasons counted per child (`REJECTED_CANARY/SCORE/BREVITY/TRAITGYM/UNCONFIRMED`).\n- **Graduation.** Fixed at cycle 16 for every child (no early stopping, so comparisons stay matched); the graduation criterion (exam slope ≈ 0 over the last 4 cycles) is *measured and reported*, not used to stop. The graduation adapter + corpus + manifest are frozen as an immutable age snapshot; Stage-3 forks are one-way descendants (COORD:834-835).\n- **Speed-parallelism check (merge-test bar, IDEAS:227-230).** Cheap version (Tier A): the merged child at cycle 16 vs a single-clone child (G1 only) at cycle 16 on the G1 exam — first derivative per wall-clock hour. Full version (Tier B, 1 seed): a serial single-clone child that rotates G1/G2/G3 for 2,560 situations (≈ 48 cycles, ~5.5 days) vs the merged child at matched situations — \"pooled ≥ equal-size serial in stability\" with no canary regression.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Evaluator classroom (intermittent testing; read-only)\n\n- **Process.** One evaluator GPU per node runs a watcher that probes every **confirmed** snapshot; results go to `evaluator/child_id/cycle_XX/` and never to the child, the clones, or the parent's playbook (Rohin's item 4, IDEAS:1393; adaptive-development caveat: humans read them during development, the confirmatory policy is frozen at 09-24).\n- **Exam battery per snapshot (ON and adapter-OFF, seeded):** G1 exam 40 eids (near and far families reported separately); G2 exam 24 codes; CompilerGym report panel v2 (12 linux programs, 2 reps); novel held-out panel (12 github programs, 2 reps); PREV on the G1/G2 exams. Probe rows never train.\n- **Instruments on every exam ledger:** trait markers (§3), ritual metrics (frozen definition), JS shift vs the previous exam, tokens- and ACTs-to-best, per-program ON−OFF table (which programs carry the mean; review minor 2).\n- **Optional secondary (never headline):** a 32B rubric scorer of review quality on 5 sampled reviews per cycle, prompt version logged; IDEAS:219-226 says LLM-judge \"good reasoning\" is weak evidence.\n- **Stage 3:** the evaluator's job is inside the deployment runner (paired probes every 64 episodes on the two held-out-by-source panels); the gate panel is probed at every sleep for the gate only.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Stages, arms, and the baseline each is compared to\n\n### Stage 1 — bootstrap (Tier B; only after the guard)\n- Blocked by Codex's STOP until a fail-closed ancestry guard exists and passes negative tests (COORD:857-882). v1/v2 are `QUARANTINE_TASK_EXPOSED`; v3 is `DEV_UNVERIFIED_PROVENANCE`; none can seed any child here.\n- **Bootstrap v4 (if built):** source = fresh G1/G2 classroom rounds run under the versioned v7c code (target-blind by construction; no CompilerGym anywhere), rendered by the AWQ-32B author with the five cue paths (META-FLOW 0.4 / REVIEW 0.15 / OPENING 0.2 / CONTRAST 0.15 / full 0.1) in the **v7 dialect** (the existing corpora's `q` fields are the old render), `acts_ok` guard, `leak_scan` over every q/a (review minor 7), ritual measured on the corpus, v2.2 write r8 2 epochs, manifest with source receipts. Disclosed as 32B-authored (review M13), never as \"the child asking itself\".\n- **Success criterion (IDEAS:1372):** a bootstrapped child in its first 64 G1 situations with no critic shows a review-marker rate ≥ 2× the none-control **and** later ritual onset than controls, in ≥ 3/3 seeds.\n\n### Stage 2 — parenting childhoods (unit = child)\n| Child condition | Clones | Critic | Seeds | Tier |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **C-P** parented | G1+G2+G3 merged | present, ladder + fading | 3 | A |\n| **C-N** gyms without critic | G1+G2+G3 merged | none (world feedback only) | 3 | A (first to cut) |\n| **C-S** single-clone (G1 only) | 1 | present | 1 | A (speed check) |\n| **C-B** bootstrap v4 + parented | G1+G2+G3 | present | 3 | B |\n| **C-L** long childhood (32 cycles) | G1+G2+G3 | present | 1 | B (horizon warning) |\n| **C-Ser** serial rotation, matched situations | 1 | present | 1 | B |\n\nStage-2 baselines: adapter-OFF at every exam; PREV at every gate; C-P vs C-N isolates the critic; C-P vs C-S isolates the merge.\n\n### Stage 3 — deployment in the novel family, parent absent (unit = deployment life, forks paired by child)\n| Cell | Start adapter | Writes | Text memory | Seeds | Compared to |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **U0** (text-memory agent, review M4) | none (frozen model) | off | brief + RECALL over own ledger | 3 | frozen OFF probes; U1 |\n| **U1** (unparented learner) | none | on (gated) | same | 3 | U0; P1 |\n| **P0** (parented, frozen at graduation) | C-P graduation | off | same | 3 | U0 (entry competence, not learning-to-learn) |\n| **P1** (parented learner) | C-P graduation | on (gated) | same | 3 | P0 (paired by child); U1; N1 |\n| **N1** (gyms-only learner) | C-N graduation | on (gated) | same | 3 | P1 (critic's marginal value) |\n| **B1** (bootstrap + parented learner) | C-B graduation | on | same | 3 | P1 |\n\n- Runner: `run_life_v7` in single-clone mode on the github-v0 train list (1,024 episodes, sleep/32, probes/64 on the novel held-out panel and report panel v2, gate on panel v1); no parent process exists. P1's cumulative corpus = Stage-2 corpus ∪ Stage-3 rows (the deployed agent sleeps on pooled + own experience, IDEAS:1393 item 5), capped at 4,000 training views.\n- The **U0 arm requires the brief-refresh fix** in the runner (today arm A never refreshes its wake brief after a sleep) and RECALL over the life ledger (already how wakes work, `batch_loop.py:82-83`). It is labelled \"brief + keyword RECALL text-memory agent\", not `ACTIVE_TEXT_FIXED` (64_:59-84 has no code).\n- The frozen model without text memory is the OFF side of every paired probe; no separate life is needed.\n- Excluded from this design (other designers' lanes): k > 1 branching arms, conversations→opinions, plasticity (deferred, IDEAS:1371). The coordinator takes `k` as a per-clone parameter so a branching sibling can reuse it; k=1 everywhere here so that thinking topology is not confounded with the curriculum.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Measurement protocol and falsifiers\n\n**Protocol.** Consolidation cycle = one sleep = 32 situations per clone (Stage 2) / 32 episodes (Stage 3). Paired ON/OFF probes, seeded, BOOTSTRAP-only context, fresh probe ledger; wall-clock `alive` frozen in probe prompts and `seed` forwarded in `model_backend.__call__` (review M3). Unit of analysis: child (Stage 2), deployment life (Stage 3), forks paired by child. Harm = paired ON−OFF < −0.03. Report cell counts (k/K seeds), never narrative; every number names its child, panel, comparison arm, and the COORD SEQ entry it comes from. Both ritual definitions reported. Same generous budget for all arms (token-sufficient regime, IDEAS:312-318); tokens/ACTs logged as diagnostics.\n\n| # | Claim the sentence would make | Where / when | Metric | Margin and K | Baseline today | If it fails |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| F1 | **Learning-to-learn** (headline; programme target) | novel held-out panel, Stage-3 cycles 4→16 | [D(P1,16)−D(P1,4)] − [D(U1,16)−D(U1,4)] | ≥ 0.03 in ≥ 3/3 seed pairs; also (P1−P0)−(U1−U0) at cycle 16 ≥ 0.03 | disjoint base 0.257; on-panel transfer −0.005 to −0.008 (R2 seed2) | report curves; \"not at this scale\" with the horizon caveat |\n| F2 | Parenting improves **efficiency** | novel panel probes, cycles 9–16 | ACTs-to-best and tokens-to-best | P1 below U1 by ≥ 20% in ≥ 3/3 | untaught late 6.5–9.2 chunks-to-best | no efficiency sentence |\n| F3 | Traits **transfer** (mechanism check) | novel panel probes, cycles 9–16 | backtracks/situation; distinct ACTs after last improvement | P1 ≥ 2× U1 in ≥ 3/3 | → 0.00–0.03 late in every arm | traits are gym-local rituals; say so |\n| F4 | **Critic adds value** beyond gyms | novel panel, cycles 9–16 mean | D(P1) − D(N1) | ≥ 0.03 in ≥ 3/3 | none | \"the critic adds nothing measurable\"; keep gyms |\n| F5 | Curriculum **instils in-domain** | Stage-2 exams at cycle 16 | G1 exam ON−OFF; G2 open-rate ON−OFF | ≥ +0.06 (G1) and ≥ +0.15 (G2) in ≥ 3/3 children; far families ≥ half the near-family gain | classroom writes: > +0.03 in 8/27, < −0.03 in 8/27 (noise-level) | Stage 3 transfer is moot; report |\n| F6 | Merged childhood **ritualizes later** | per-clone ritual windows, Stage 2 | first window with ≥ 2 flags (frozen def.) | later than 224 situations-equivalent in ≥ 3/3 children, ≥ 2/3 clones | onset 160–224 in 8/9 | multi-gym merge does not delay ritual |\n| F7 | **Speed parallelism** pays | G1 exam at matched wall-clock | merged C-P vs single-clone C-S at cycle 16 | merged ≥ single by ≥ 0.06, no canary regression | none | merge buys throughput but not learning; Tier-B serial test decides |\n| F8 | **No harm / gate safety** | report panel v2 and novel panel, all Stage-3 probes | lives with ≥ 1 harmful pair | gated arms 0/3 harmful and every final ≥ base − 0.03 on both panels, else no safety sentence; catches reported from the rejection log regardless | ungated 4/9; gated 0/6 post-selection | report rejection log only |\n| F9 | Entry competence ≠ learning | novel panel, cycle 1 | D(P0,1) − D(U0,1) | reported, not a claim; if ≥ 0.03 while F1 fails, the curriculum taught competence, not learning-to-learn | none | wording follows the cells |\n| F10 | Bootstrap installs the habit | first 64 G1 situations, no critic | review-marker rate; ritual onset | ≥ 2× none-control and later onset, ≥ 3/3 | bootstraps unmeasured | bootstrap stays future work |\n| F11 | Day-one gate | every clone, cycles 2–6 | JS first-ACT shift | > 0.10 at least once per clone | 0.02–0.13 | behaviour did not change; stop and inspect before Stage 3 |\n| F12 | Repetition without the parent's text | Stage-2 clones after the critic fades | uncued enactment rate (changed next action + improved outcome) | ≥ the pre-fade rate in ≥ 2/3 children | echo 0.454 vs 0.354 controls (lexical only) | child rehearsal depends on the text; fading schedule revisited |\n\nNoise rulers: SD(OFF) 0.009, SD(paired ON−OFF) ≈ 0.013, same-adapter replicate SD 0.0065 with a heavy tail (one pair 0.065) → margins of 0.03 on 2-rep 12-program panels averaged over 8 cycles; rule-game 0.03–0.044 at 40 eids → 0.06; LockGym band measured before reading.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Split hygiene, provenance, contamination\n\n- **Held out by source, not identifier.** Gate = {npb, blas, opencv, tensorflow}; report = {linux}; novel = {github} with an in-source held-out panel; parenting-side CompilerGym = {cbench−8, chstone, mibench, poj104}. Overlap is checked by dataset **and** by reset-state IR instruction count/hash; a 67×N overlap table is published (F2).\n- **Parent target-blind** on every panel and on Stage 3 (absent). **Bootstrap gym-free** (G1/G2 only). **Per-source provenance** in every merged row so \"did the G3 clone's experience carry the gain\" is ablatable by retraining the graduation corpus without `env_id = G3` (one extra write per child, Tier B) — provenance kept as a hygiene record; per-source attribution is not a claim requirement (IDEAS:1399 reconciled with 1396).\n- **Provenance manifest (fail-closed; Codex COORD:837-842)** written at birth and at every commit and fork: child base snapshot hash (a09a3545…), adapter_config sha, mechanism_version = hash of `organism_v6/*.py` + gym code, writer/compiler path, corpus sha and per-row source ledger shas, bootstrap source set (or none), parent model + revision + `SCANNER_VERSION` + prompt versions, exposure_domains (e.g. `[rulegame_v2, lockgym, compilergym_train_persistence]`), ancestor chain with `QUARANTINE_*` flags. `admit()` refuses launch on any missing field or quarantined ancestor; negative tests: copied directory without receipts → refuse; self-declared manifest → refuse; ancestor marked `QUARANTINE_TASK_EXPOSED` → refuse. Every child in this design carries the record from day one (contamination rule (b), IDEAS:1346) even if Stage 1 never runs.\n- **Contamination discipline.** Parenting comparisons (C-P vs C-N vs C-B) are between clean children born from the same base with matched seeds; Stage-3 forks are disposable descendants; nothing from a Stage-3 life returns to any child, parent, or protocol (including selection decisions). Development pilots (09-23–09-24) are labelled development; the confirmatory launch runs under the frozen mechanism hash.\n- **Hygiene fixes carried in:** persisted program lists; `seed` forwarded in single calls; `alive` frozen in probe prompts; migrate script carries all flags; hostnames redacted from anything cited.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Compute and calendar\n\n### Throughput assumptions (state them, then measure in the pilot)\n- CompilerGym clone: ~18 situations/h under no-DONE (today 22 eps/h with DONE; base uses ~13 of 16 chunks, so ≤ 20% slower). G1/G2: ~35 situations/h (budget 12, short prompts, cheap evaluate).\n- Stage-2 cycle ≈ 1.8 h wake + 0.75 h sleep (write ~35 min, gate probe ~8 min, canaries ~5 min, engine reloads) ≈ 2.55 h; **16 cycles ≈ 41 h per child**; 3 clone GPUs held throughout.\n- Stage-3 life: 1,024 episodes ≈ 57 h + probes (16 pairs × 48 episodes = 768 ≈ 43 h) + writes (32 × ~30 min = 16 h for writing arms) → **~116 h (writing) / ~100 h (frozen)**. Fallback if quota-limited: 512-episode Stage 3 with probes every 32 (≈ 71 h).\n\n### GPU-hours\n| Item | GPU-h |\n|---|---|\n| Panels/splits precheck (node CPU), noise bands G1/G2 (8 reps each), frozen-model references on all panels ×3 | 10 |\n| v7 smoke (2 GPUs × 2 h), 2-clone merge smoke (4 × 4 h), text-memory smoke, guard tests | 30 |\n| Pilot child (3 clones + parent + evaluator, 4 cycles ≈ 10 h) + 64-episode Stage-3 smoke | 60 |\n| Stage 2 Tier A: 6 children × 3 clones × 41 h + 3 node-waves × (parent + evaluator) 82 h + C-S 41 h | 1,030 |\n| Stage 3 Tier A: 9 writing lives × 116 h + 6 frozen lives × 100 h | 1,650 |\n| Overhead 15% (reloads, restarts, failed cycles) | 400 |\n| **Tier A total** | **≈ 3,000** (rounded down from 3,180 by trimming N1 probes to 1 rep if needed) |\n| Tier B: bootstrap v4 (classroom source rounds ~70, rendering ~8, training minutes) | 100 |\n| Tier B: C-B 3 children (Stage 2) + B1 3 lives | 800 |\n| Tier B: C-L long childhood (1), C-Ser serial (1) + its Stage 3, G3-ablation retrains | 400 |\n| **Grand total** | **≈ 4,300** |\n\n### Colossus lease reality\nQuota is a rolling trailing-30-day window of lease-hours (booked, not utilized), 1,440 h; the two current leases (node 1 to 09-14, node 2 to ~09-21) remain inside the window until mid-October, so **no headroom returns by 09-19 on its own**. Action on 09-11: request a quota increase through Colossus Support for three 8×A40 nodes 09-21→10-10 (≈ 1,450 node-hours) plus the trailing usage.
