Decision points that pending data resolves\n\n| Pending data | Outcome | Design change |\n|---|---|---|\n| **F1b disjoint re-probe** (panel v1, seeded 4242; running) | every gated final ≥ base − 0.03 | gate design (panel v1 + PREV + behaviour) carries into v7c unchanged; a gated-safety sentence is allowed for the old paper only |\n| | any gated final < base − 0.03 | gate keeps its structure but **provisional commit + confirmation become mandatory**, and safety is reported only via the rejection log (F8 wording) |\n| | on-panel gain shows ≈ 0 transfer (like R2 seed2) | confirms recipe lock-in as the whole on-panel gain; keeps the **recipe-fails (h ≥ 0.10) selection** for every v7c panel; nothing else changes |\n| | per-program SD across adapters < 0.005 on the three tensorflow programs | drop them from the gate panel (they carry no information); else keep and flag |\n| **M4 brief-only proxy** (`brief_baseline.sh`, not yet run) | OFF+brief ≈ ON (brief carries the gain) | expect small U1−U0 deltas → raise K for U0/U1 to 5 seeds (Tier B budget); paper frame drops weights-vs-tokens; U0 remains the mandatory baseline |\n| | ON ≥ OFF+brief + 0.02 | the weights carry something beyond the brief; K = 3 stands |\n| | not run by 09-14 | limitations sentence in the paper; **no change here** — U0 is a full life in this design regardless |\n| **R5** (gate on panel v1, no parent; seed 700 launched) | report-panel ON−OFF ≥ 0 and 0 harmful at 1024 | disjoint gate becomes the default for every v7c life (as designed) |\n| | harmful pairs appear on the report panel | the old \"0 harmful\" was post-selection; F8 wording locks to the rejection log; confirmation step mandatory |\n| | rejection rate on panel v1 | sets the Stage-2 sleep-time budget (each rejection costs a probe) and whether the gate probe uses 1 or 2 reps |\n| | Codex ratifies floor-over-confirmed + provisional commit | gate spec as in §5; if refused, revert to \"floor = max(gate_base, best committed)\" without confirmation and say so |\n| **R4** (gate + parent v3, 7 lives, young) | contrast/backtrack markers stay above untaught controls at 1024 (node-1 R4 rose 5.2→10.0 at n=3, ≤ 380 eps) | the pattern parent (sleep-time brief) is kept as a second critic channel under the fading rule |\n| | markers converge to ~0 like every other arm | pattern parent demoted to a monitor; the per-situation critic is the only parent channel |\n| | efficiency does not separate (today: not met) | F2 keeps the 20% margin but moves to the novel panel; expectation stated as \"unmet today\" |\n| | conversation/v7 trials on R4 children | development-only (firewall); no data from them enters v7c |\n| **Split hygiene ratification** (IDEAS:1396, Codex) | ratified | G3 (CompilerGym persistence split) is a Stage-2 clone; G3-ablation retrain in Tier B |\n| | not ratified | G3 → fourth target-blind gym (landscape as its own clone); CompilerGym only at Stage 3; F1 unchanged |\n| **Provenance guard** (Codex schema + Fable implementation + negative tests) | passes by 09-26 | bootstrap v4 source rounds start 09-26; C-B/B1 in Tier B |\n| | not by 09-26 | Stage 1 deferred; all v7c children still carry the manifest |\n| **Fable's answers to Codex's three challenges** (branch SLEEP; opinions; fork vs clone) | committed-branch-only adopted | the merged compile already implements it (eligibility = situations with a committed ACT); JOIN syntheses would be eligible under the same rule |\n| **Ritual recomputation under both definitions (M6)** | definitions disagree on RP onsets | Stage-2 ritual reported under both; the fading trigger uses the frozen definition only |\n| **Leak audit of delivered briefs (M8)** | residual leaks found | per-gym `extra_terms` extended before launch; critic wording stays \"process-only by prompt and leak-scanned\" |\n| **M10 absorption rescoring** | any | instrument only: absorption probe matches the v2.2 writer here |\n\n---\n\n## 12. Ratifications requested and defaults (nothing here is authorized by this document)\n\n**Rohin:** (1) v7c as a new lineage root with the v2.2 writer; (2) Stage 2 = 16 fixed cycles, 3 clones (G1, G2, G3), 2 children per node; (3) the 2×2 + N1 arm set and 3 seeds; (4) the F1 margin 0.03 and K = 3/3; (5) the fading rule as the reconciliation of repetition and fading; (6) the quota request; (7) whether N1 or Tier B is cut first if compute is short (default: N1 deferred, Tier B after).\n\n**Codex:** (a) split hygiene (G3 in Stage 2); (b) gate definition (floor over confirmed; provisional commit); (c) near-dup equivalence operationalized as env + ACT set + outcome class + Jaccard ≥ 0.8 with support retained; (d) the 4,000-view training cap as a controlled replay dose; (e) provenance manifest schema and negative tests; (f) v2.2 writer for the root; (g) the leak-scan term sets per gym and `SCANNER_VERSION`; (h) the Stage-3 unit (deployment life, forks paired by child) and Fisher/cluster-bootstrap reporting.\n\nDefaults if unanswered by 09-24 are **not** applied silently: the pilot proceeds as development; the confirmatory launch on 09-25 waits for Rohin's ratification of the frozen hash.\n\n---\n\n## 13. Risks and mitigations\n\n1. **Quota/leases.** Rolling window leaves no headroom on 09-19 without an increase → file 09-11; 2-node fallback (28 days, N1 deferred); 512-episode Stage-3 fallback.\n2. **v7 stream teething** (idle loops, format drift without DONE, budget gaming) → smoke + pilot; fallback = v6 stream with DONE for the curriculum (the curriculum does not depend on no-DONE; only the REVIEW white space does, which then becomes a harness-injected \"[REVIEW]\" chunk after DONE).\n3. **Dataset installability** (github-v0 / linux-v0 / poj104-v1 offline) → checked 09-19; fallback split in §3.\n4. **Write time growth** under merged corpora → view cap, measured in the pilot; v1 bare-text fallback chosen before launch, never mid-lineage.\n5. **Exam noise** → margins from measured bands; LockGym band measured first; 2 reps on CompilerGym panels.\n6. **Near vs far transfer** (62_) → held-out families inside G1/G2 and a source-disjoint novel family; F3/F5 separate near from far.\n7. **Gym-specific rituals** (fixed probe sequences in G1, fixed opening guesses in G2) → per-clone ritual windows, F6, and the critic's ladder; the merge is the hypothesized delay mechanism, tested not assumed.\n8. **Leaks through the critic** (rule descriptors, lock digits, pass names) → per-gym terms, FALLBACK, standing audit, fallback rate reported per child.\n9. **Parent-server throughput** (~320 critic calls per cycle per node) → thread pool as in classroom_round; measured in the pilot; if > 15 min per cycle, critique every second situation for G1/G2.\n10. **Adaptive-development bias** → exam results are read during the pilot only; the confirmatory policy (prompts, ladder thresholds, fading rule, gate) is frozen by hash on 09-24 (Codex item 1).\n11. **Provenance drift** (a fork resumed from the wrong snapshot) → `admit()` at every launch; manifests hashed; disposable Stage-3 descendants never re-entered.\n12. **Over-claiming** → every sentence in the digest is a row of the falsifier table with k/K; reversals named; the \"killer metric\" stays the programme's target unless F1 passes at 3/3.\n\n---\n\n## 14. Interfaces to the other designers\n\n- **From the v7 stream (designers 2/4):** `EpisodeDriver`-compatible situation loop with no DONE; `[SITUATION]`/`[STATE k]`/`[REVIEW]` markers; `situation_id`, `state_id` (and `branch_id`, unused here) on every ledger row; a per-gym seal policy hook (`gym.sealed(episode, state) -> bool`); a `k` parameter (fixed 1 here). If branching ships, it runs as a mechanism sibling forked from the same clean base with its own falsifiers; it must not be added to C-P mid-lineage.\n- **To the compile designer:** the eligibility rule (committed-ACT situations only; REVIEW chunks of such situations; no parent/restate/exam rows), provenance fields, strata, cap, and the near-dup equivalence — so one compile path serves single lives, merged clones, and later branch JOINs.\n- **To the measurement designer:** `trait_markers` field list (§3), the falsifier table (§8), the unit rules (§8), and the panel JSON schema (recipe/-Oz/-O3 per program, IR hash, frozen-model ×3).\n"
}
